# Features

---

## Search & Filter

The whole feature tree on one page — search by name, filter by type, class, level, action, keyword or condition, then jump straight to the card you need.

*The **Condition** filter matches every ability whose rules text mentions that condition — whether it inflicts, requires or removes it. Flavor text doesn't count.*

If you are midair when the drake's rampage ends, you take no damage from the fall. Additionally, you can use the drake's Drake Breath maneuver until their rampage ends.","href":"companion/beastheart/drake/level-6/a-burning-inside-me/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"A Champion's Cry","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":6,"action":"main","keywords":["Area","Champion","Magic"],"conditions":["frightened"],"distance":"**3** burst","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"Your champion unleashes a bellow that shakes you to your core.","href":"ability/summoner/level-6/a-champions-cry/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"A Conversation With Fire","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Fire","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"When you spend 1 uninterrupted minute in front of a fire, you can speak the name of another creature. If that creature is willing to speak to you, their image appears in the fire, and they can see you before them in a shimmering ball of light. The two of you can speak to each other through these images as if you were together in person. As a maneuver, you or the creature can end the conversation.","href":"elementalist/level-3/a-conversation-with-fire/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"A Muse's Muse","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"At the start of each of your turns during combat, you gain 1d3 + 1 drama instead of 1d3.","href":"troubadour/level-7/a-muses-muse/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"A Sense for Truth","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Exorcist","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"You are trained in secret techniques from your order that allow you to discern the truth with supernatural precision. If a creature is of a lower level than you, you automatically know when they are lying, though you don't necessarily know the actual truth behind their lie. Additionally, you gain an edge on tests made to detect lies or hidden motives.","href":"censor/level-2/a-sense-for-truth/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"A Squad Unto Myself","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"5 Discipline","keywords":["Area","Psionic","Weapon"],"distance":"**2** burst","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"You move so quickly, it seems as though an army assaulted your foes.","href":"ability/null/level-1/a-squad-unto-myself/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"A Step Ahead","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Reaver","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"You move with legendary grace. Whenever you make an Agility test, you can roll three dice and choose which two to use. Additionally, whenever you use the Disengage move action, the distance you can shift gains a bonus equal to your Agility score.","href":"fury/level-8/a-step-ahead/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Absolute Zero","klass":"Null","subclass":"Cryokinetic","source":"class","level":9,"action":"maneuver","cost":"11 Discipline","keywords":["Psionic"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"You become the coldest thing in the timescape.","href":"ability/null/level-9/absolute-zero/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Absorb","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Companion","Melee","Weapon"],"conditions":["grabbed"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One enemy","flavor":"With a sickening squelch, the ball oozes around their hapless prey.","href":"ability/companion/beastheart/gummy-ball/level-1/absorb/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Absorption Field","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Discipline","keywords":["Psionic"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"Your null field absorbs kinetic energy.","href":"ability/null/level-3/absorption-field/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Abyssal Evolution","klass":"Summoner","subclass":"Blight","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"At the start of each of your turns, you can transform up to two of your demon minions within your Summoner's Range. A demon can transform into a different demon minion within your Summoner's Range, maintaining their current Stamina. Starting from round 2, a demon can transform any demon minion you can call forth for half the essence cost, as if you summoned the new minion into the demon's space.","href":"summoner/level-8/abyssal-evolution/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Accelerate","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Chronopathy","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","keywords":["Psionic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self or one creature","flavor":"To your ally, it seems as though the world has slowed down.","href":"ability/talent/level-1/accelerate/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Accelerate","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Chronopathy","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following ability.","grants":"the Accelerate maneuver","href":"talent/level-1/accelerate/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Acceleration **5**","targets":"Three allies","flavor":"You forcibly stuff more moments into a critical point in time, knowing full well you might need to steal some of your own.","href":"ability/talent/level-9/acceleration-field/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Acrobatics","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Duelist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"none","keywords":["Area","Magic","Performance"],"distance":"**5** aura","targets":"Self and each ally in the area","flavor":"Folks love a good tumble.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-1/acrobatics/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Acrobatics","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Duelist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have Hero","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":5,"action":"main","cost":"9 Drama","keywords":["Area","Melee","Weapon"],"distance":"**3** burst","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"You wield your weapon at blistering speed, leaving everyone around you fighting for their lives.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-5/action-hero/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Adding and Subtracting Actions","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Although you and your companion share your action economy, you are treated as separate creatures for effects that grant or remove main actions, maneuvers, and move actions. For instance, the talent's Applied Chronometrics ability grants an extra maneuver. If both you and your companion are targeted by the ability, you both gain extra maneuvers.","href":"beastheart/level-1/adding-and-subtracting-actions/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Advance","source":"other","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"When a creature takes the Advance move action, they move a number of squares up to their speed. They can break up this movement with their maneuver and main action however they wish.","href":"common/move-actions/advance/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Advanced Tactics","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Insurgent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"triggered","keywords":["Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One ally","flavor":"Your leadership aids an ally.","href":"ability/tactician/level-1/advanced-tactics/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Afflict a Bountiful Decay","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Green","Magic","Ranged","Rot","Strike"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Your curse causes your foe's flesh to rot off as spores that aid your allies.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-1/afflict-a-bountiful-decay/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Again","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Chronopathy","source":"class","level":1,"action":"triggered","keywords":["Psionic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self or one creature","flavor":"You step back a split second to see if things play out a little differently.","href":"ability/talent/level-1/again/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Again","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Chronopathy","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following ability.","grants":"the Again triggered action","href":"talent/level-1/again/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Aid Attack","source":"other","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"A creature who uses the Aid Attack maneuver chooses an enemy adjacent to them. The next ability roll an ally makes against that enemy before the start of the aiding creature's next turn gains an edge.","href":"common/maneuvers/aid-attack/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Alacrity of the Heart","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Love","source":"class","level":9,"action":"maneuver","cost":"11 Piety","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One ally","flavor":"You speak inspiring words to a friend and spur them to incredible feats.","href":"ability/conduit/level-9/alacrity-of-the-heart/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"All Is a Feather","klass":"Hakaan","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"You are exceptionally strong. You gain an edge on tests made to lift and haul heavy objects.","href":"trait/hakaan/all-is-a-feather/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"All of You Versus All of Me","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","cost":"5 Ferocity","keywords":["Area","Beastheart"],"conditions":["taunted"],"distance":"**3** burst","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"Let all of them come forward and shatter themselves against your might!","href":"ability/beastheart/level-1/all-of-you-versus-all-of-me/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Allow Me to Introduce Tonight's Players","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you take the first turn in a combat encounter, you can use a main action to introduce yourself and your allies to your opponents. Each ally can shift up to their speed, and ability rolls made against them have a double bane until the end of the combat round. Additionally, any surprised enemy is no longer surprised.","href":"troubadour/level-2/allow-me-to-introduce-tonights-players/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Among Our Ranks","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"As a respite activity, you summon a willing and not-restrained NPC or player ally to join your party, regardless of distance or manifold. The target stays until the start of your next respite or until they are killed, in which they are immediately dismissed to the place from which they were summoned. You can't have more than one character summoned in this way.","href":"summoner/level-10/among-our-ranks/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Ancestral Memory","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"Each time you finish a respite, you can choose a number of skills you have up to your Reason score and replace them with an equal number of skills from the interpersonal and lore skill groups. These replacements last until the end of your next respite.","href":"talent/level-7/ancestral-memory/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Animal Form: Bear","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"While you are in your bear form, your size is 2 and you gain a +1 bonus to distance with melee weapon abilities.","href":"fury/boren/animal-form-bear/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Animal Form: Crow","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"While you are in your crow form, your size is 1T and you can fly. You can use the Hide maneuver as a free maneuver, and you can use your allies as cover when you hide. You can't use any abilities while in this form except for Aspect of the Wild.","href":"fury/corven/animal-form-crow/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Animal Form: Rat","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"While you are in your rat form, your size is 1T and you can automatically climb at full speed while moving. You can use the Hide maneuver as a free maneuver, you can use your allies as cover when you hide, and you can stay hidden while you move through squares occupied by any creature. Additionally, you gain an edge on tests made to climb other creatures. You can't use any abilities while in this form except for Aspect of the Wild.","href":"fury/raden/animal-form-rat/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Animal Form: Wolf","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"While you are in your wolf form, your size is 1L, you have a +2 bonus to speed, and you ignore difficult terrain.","href":"fury/vuken/animal-form-wolf/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Anticipating Strike","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":5,"action":"triggered","cost":"9 Discipline","keywords":["Melee","Psionic","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["restrained"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You suddenly strike an enemy, then grab them in a psionically enhanced grip.","href":"ability/null/level-5/anticipating-strike/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Anticipation","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Mastermind","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have learned to be more preemptive on the battlefield, thinking more steps ahead than your opponents. You can target two creatures with your Mark ability.","href":"tactician/level-5/anticipation/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Apex Predator","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Ferocity","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["slowed"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"I will hunt you down.","href":"ability/fury/level-2/apex-predator/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Apostate","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Paragon","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Wrath","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You channel holy energy to seal an enemy's fate.","href":"ability/censor/level-9/apostate/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Appeal to the Muses","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"Before you roll to gain drama at the start of your turn, you can make your appeal (no action required). If you do, your roll gains the following additional effects:","href":"troubadour/level-2/appeal-to-the-muses/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Applause","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have an epic resource called applause. Each time you finish a respite, you gain applause equal to the XP you gain. You can spend applause on your abilities as if it were drama.","href":"troubadour/level-10/applause/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Applied Chronometrics","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Chronopathy","source":"class","level":2,"action":"maneuver","cost":"5 Clarity","keywords":["Chronopathy","Psionic","Ranged"],"conditions":["dazed"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Special","flavor":"Time slows down around you. burst","targets":"Self and each ally in the area","flavor":"Your champion's appearance has an enchanting impact on you and your allies.","href":"ability/summoner/level-6/armys-idol/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Arrest","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"5 bitch.","href":"ability/censor/level-1/arrest/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Arrestor Cycle","klass":"Null","subclass":"Chronokinetic","source":"class","level":9,"action":"triggered","cost":"11 over again.","href":"ability/null/level-9/arrestor-cycle/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Artful objects","flavor":"And they said practicing fencing was a waste!","href":"ability/troubadour/level-1/artful-flourish/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Aspect Whenever you pull a creature adjacent to you and that creature has M \u003c AVERAGE, you can use a free triggered action to make that creature grabbed by you.","href":"fury/boren/aspect-benefits/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Aspect Benefits","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"You gain an edge on tests made to hide and sneak. Additionally, whenever you fall, you can use a free triggered action to use your Aspect of the Wild ability.","href":"fury/corven/aspect-benefits/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Aspect Benefits","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"You gain an edge on tests made to hide and sneak. Additionally, you ignore difficult terrain.","href":"fury/raden/aspect-benefits/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Aspect Benefits","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you use the Knockback maneuver, you can then use the Aid Attack maneuver as a free triggered benefits are always available to you, and you gain additional benefits while in the animal or hybrid form granted by your stormwight kit.","href":"fury/stormwight-kits/aspect-benefits-and-animal-form/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Aspect of the Wild","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":0,"action":"maneuver","keywords":["Magic"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"You assume the form of the animal who channels your ferocity.","href":"ability/fury/stormwight-kits/aspect-of-the-wild/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Aspect of the Wild","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the Action","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your primordial aspect grants you a triggered action, as shown on the Aspect Triggered Actions table.","options":4,"href":"fury/level-1/aspect-triggered-action/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Assassinate","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":8,"action":"main","cost":"11 foe.","href":"ability/shadow/level-8/assassinate/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Asymmetric Warfare","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Insurgent","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have advanced your skills in subterfuge, now directing full battlefield strategy and logistics. During a montage test or negotiation, you can obtain one automatic success on a test made using a skill from the intrigue skill group. Additionally, you can use skills from the intrigue skill group to conceal large groups of people, such as escaping civilians and groups of guerilla warriors.","href":"tactician/level-7/asymmetric-warfare/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Aura of Souls","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Death","source":"class","level":6,"action":"maneuver","cost":"9 Piety","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"distance":"**4** aura","targets":"Each creature in the area","flavor":"A whirlwind of souls of the dead flies around you at your command.","href":"ability/conduit/level-6/aura-of-souls/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Auteur Features","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"Once on each of your turns, you can use a free maneuver to change one condition affecting a creature within distance of your Dramatic Monologue ability. Choose one of the following conditions on the target: bleeding, frightened, prone, slowed, or taunted. You change that condition to another of those conditions, maintaining the duration and origin of the original condition. A target who is no longer prone can stand up.","options":3,"href":"troubadour/level-5/auteur-features/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Avalanche Impact","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Berserker","source":"class","level":6,"action":"maneuver","cost":"9 Ferocity","keywords":["Magic"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"You leap and crash down, causing a shockwave that devastates foes.","href":"ability/fury/level-6/avalanche-impact/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Avalanche Rush","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Punisher","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","keywords":["Beastheart","Melee","Weapon"],"conditions":["prone"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You ride a cascade of ice over your foes.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-1/avalanche-rush/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Avatar","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"You are now an avatar of your god! When you use your Prayer feature, you can be affected by up to three prayers at once, and you can change all those prayers and your ward as a respite activity. You can also use a maneuver to activate one of your domain effects (see Domain Piety and Effects) without needing to pray.","href":"conduit/level-10/avatar/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Avatar of the Green","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":9,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your companion has transcended beasthood. Although they're still your faithful friend, they're also a vessel for nature's wisdom and memories. Your companion's Reason score increases to 1, or increases by 1 if it is already 1 or higher, and they learn every language you know. Your companion can communicate telepathically with any creature within 10 squares, using language as well as images and feelings. Additionally, you learn the Nature skill and one other skill from the lore skill group.","href":"beastheart/level-9/avatar-of-the-green/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Avoidance","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Any effect on the lightbender that would be ended by a saving throw instead ends automatically at the end of their next turn.","href":"companion/beastheart/lightbender/level-1/avoidance/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Awe","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"3 take on a new visage in their mind.","href":"ability/talent/level-1/awe/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Back Blasphemer!","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Area","Magic","Melee","Weapon"],"distance":"**2** cube within **1**","targets":"Each enemy in foes.","href":"ability/censor/level-1/back-blasphemer/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Back!","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"3 backward.","href":"ability/fury/level-1/back/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Backhand","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Companion","Melee","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee and Ambush","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Insurgent","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have trained your squad to be silent ambushers.","href":"tactician/level-8/bait-and-ambush/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Ballad of the world.","href":"ability/censor/level-9/banish/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Banshee Howl","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Guardian","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Ferocity","keywords":["Area","Companion","Magic"],"conditions":["frightened"],"distance":"**3** burst","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"Your companion's howl, screech, roar, or psychic emanation presages death to those who hear it.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-9/banshee-howl/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Barbed Tail","klass":"Devil","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your pointy tail allows you to punctuate all your actions. Once per round when you make a melee strike, you can deal extra damage with the strike equal to your highest characteristic score.","href":"trait/devil/barbed-tail/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Bastion Ward","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your god grants you a holy countenance that protects you at all times. You gain a +1 bonus to saving throws.","href":"conduit/level-1/bastion-ward/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Battle Augmentation","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can wear light armor and wield light weapons effectively, even though you don't have a kit. While you wear light armor, you gain a +3 bonus to Stamina, and that bonus increases by 3 at 4th, 7th, and 10th levels. While you wield a light weapon, you gain a +1 damage bonus with weapon abilities, including free strikes. You can use light armor treasures and light weapon treasures.","href":"talent/level-1/battle-augmentation/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Battle Cry","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","cost":"3 Focus","keywords":["Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Three allies","flavor":"You shout a phrase that galvanizes your team.","href":"ability/tactician/level-1/battle-cry/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Battle burst","targets":"Special","flavor":"Your companion shatters the floodgates that keep their rampage dammed up, and it cascades into the unprepared minds of nearby creatures.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-9/battle-frenzy/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Battle Plan","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Mastermind","source":"class","level":6,"action":"maneuver","cost":"9 Focus","keywords":["Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Three Signature abilities can be used at will. (Quick Build: Come On!)","options":2,"href":"beastheart/level-1/beastheart-abilities/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Beasthearts and Magic Treasure","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your companion can't use every consumable, but with your help, they can benefit from an edible or drinkable consumable, such as a Healing Potion. While using a kit, your companion can also benefit from a consumable that enhances a weapon, such as a Lachomp Tooth. When you use one of these kinds of consumables while you are adjacent to your companion, you can grant the benefit to your companion instead of yourself. You must take the action type required to use the consumable; no action is required of your companion.","options":3,"href":"beastheart/level-1/beasthearts-and-magic-treasure/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Become the Beast","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your companion's rampage burns in your brain, and you become part beast yourself. block.","href":"beastheart/level-6/become-the-beast/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Begone!","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Exorcist","source":"class","level":6,"action":"main","cost":"9 Wrath","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"distance":"**3** burst","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"You terrify your enemies into retreating, creating chaos in their ranks.","href":"ability/censor/level-6/begone/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Behold a Shield of Faith!","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"3 Wrath","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"A mighty blow turns your foe's vitality into a holy light that envelops you and an ally, discouraging enemies who might attack you.","href":"ability/censor/level-1/behold-a-shield-of-faith/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Behold the Face of Chaos","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Prowler","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Ferocity","keywords":["Companion","Magic","Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["frightened"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Your companion appears next to their victim You are not prone after falling and landing on another creature.","href":"fury/level-5/bounder/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Breath","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have an epic resource called breath. For the purpose of effects that end at the end of the companion's turn, or any other rules elements that depend on the start or end of creature's turn, the start and end of your turn is also the start and end of the companion's turn.","href":"beastheart/level-1/companion-rules/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Compulsion","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Telepathy","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you obtain a success on a test using a skill from the interpersonal skill group while interacting with an NPC, you can ask them a question using your Telepathic Speech feature. Death!","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Ferocity","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["dazed","frightened"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Your unbridled rage strikes terror in their hearts.","href":"ability/fury/level-2/death-death/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Debilitating Strike","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":5,"action":"main","cost":"9 Ferocity","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["slowed"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You need just one blow to sabotage your target.","href":"ability/fury/level-5/debilitating-strike/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Defend","source":"other","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"When a creature takes the Defend main action, ability rolls made against them have a double bane until the start of their next turn. With the Director's permission, you can also create your own deity and choose four domains to be part of their portfolio.","href":"conduit/level-1/deity-and-domains/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Deleted Scene","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Auteur","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever a creature within distance of your Dramatic Monologue ability makes a power roll, you can spend 1 drama as a free triggered action to use Dramatic Monologue, targeting only one creature.","href":"troubadour/level-8/deleted-scene/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Deluge","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Stormwight","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Ferocity","keywords":["Area","Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"**5** cube within **10**","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"You summon your primordial storm.","href":"ability/fury/level-9/deluge/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Demon Door","klass":"Shadow","subclass":"Black Ash","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Insight","keywords":["Magic","Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["grabbed"],"distance":"Melee **3**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You create a temporary portal to allow a massive demonic hand to reach through.","href":"ability/shadow/level-9/demon-door/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Demon Unleashed","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Ferocity","keywords":["Magic"],"conditions":["frightened"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"Foes tremble at the sight of you.","href":"ability/fury/level-3/demon-unleashed/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Demonologist","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Exorcist","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"The most esoteric secrets of your order teach you that to defeat your enemy, you must understand them. You treat your Renown as 2 higher than usual when dealing with demons, devils, and other agents of chaos. If you successfully complete a negotiation with one of these creatures, you gain an edge on power rolls made against them and can use your Judgment ability against them as a free triggered action before an encounter begins.","href":"censor/level-8/demonologist/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Density Augmentation","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You gain a +6 bonus to Stamina, and this bonus increases by 6 at 4th, 7th, and 10th levels. Additionally, you gain a +1 bonus to stability.","href":"null/level-1/density-augmentation/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Density Augmentation","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You gain a +6 bonus to Stamina, and this bonus increases by 6 at 4th, 7th, and 10th levels. Additionally, you gain a +1 bonus to stability.","href":"talent/level-1/density-augmentation/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Determination","klass":"Human","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"A tolerance for pain and distress allows you to push through difficult situations. If you are frightened, slowed, or weakened, you can use a maneuver to immediately end one of those conditions.","href":"trait/human/determination/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Detonate Sigil","klass":"Revenant","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Magic","Ranged","Strike"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature bearing your sigil","flavor":"A magic sigil you placed on a creature explodes with energy.","href":"ability/revenant/detonate-sigil/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Devil Traits","klass":"Devil","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Devil heroes have access to the following traits.","options":2,"href":"trait/devil/devil-traits/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Dire Wolf","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"While the wolf is rampaging, you and the wolf are surrounded by an aura of dread, and enemies who start their turn adjacent to you or the wolf who have P \u003c STRONG are frightened until the end of their next turn.","href":"companion/beastheart/wolf/level-10/dire-wolf/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Disciple of Earth","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Earth","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your body is strengthened by your mind's connection to the element of permanence. You have a +6 bonus to Stamina, and you gain an additional +3 bonus to Stamina whenever you gain a level past 2nd.","href":"elementalist/level-2/disciple-of-earth/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Disciple of Fire","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Fire","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your connection to fire allows you to protect yourself from it, even as you rip away the protections of others. You have fire immunity equal to 5 plus your level. Additionally, fire damage you deal ignores a target's fire immunity.","href":"elementalist/level-2/disciple-of-fire/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Disciple of the Green","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can use a maneuver to shapeshift into a type of creature on the Green Animal Forms table. While in animal form, you can speak, and you use your Reason score to make melee free strikes. Your statistics stay the same except as noted on the table.","options":1,"href":"elementalist/level-2/disciple-of-the-green/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Discipline","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"As your mastery of your body and tradition grows, it imbues you with a Heroic Resource called discipline.","options":2,"href":"null/level-1/discipline/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Discipline Mastery","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"As you advance in your chosen null tradition, you gain certain benefits in combat, including benefits based on the amount of discipline you have. Benefits based on how much discipline you have last until the end of your turn, even if a benefit would become unavailable to you because of the amount of discipline you spend during your turn.","options":3,"href":"null/level-1/discipline-mastery/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Discipline Mastery Improvement","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your Discipline Mastery feature provides additional benefits when you have 12 or more discipline.","href":"null/level-10/discipline-mastery-improvement/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Discipline Mastery Improvement","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your Discipline Mastery feature provides additional benefits when you have 8 or more discipline.","href":"null/level-4/discipline-mastery-improvement/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Discipline Mastery Improvement","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your Discipline Mastery feature provides additional benefits when you have 10 or more discipline.","href":"null/level-7/discipline-mastery-improvement/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Disengage","source":"other","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"When a creature takes the Disengage move action, they can shift 1 square. Certain class features, kits, and other rules allow a creature to shift more than 1 square when they disengage. A creature who does so can break up their shift with their maneuver and main action however they wish.","href":"common/move-actions/disengage/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Disorienting Strike","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"3 Insight","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Your attack leaves them reeling, allowing you to follow up.","href":"ability/shadow/level-1/disorienting-strike/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Distance Augmentation","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your ranged psionic abilities gain a +2 bonus to distance.","href":"talent/level-1/distance-augmentation/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Distance Is Only Memory","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Void","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"Each time you finish a respite, you can open a two-way portal that leads to any place you have previously been. You and your allies can pass through the portal, which remains open for 1 hour or until you dismiss it as a main action.","href":"elementalist/level-3/distance-is-only-memory/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Distortion Temporal","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Chronopathy","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"While you are not dying, time behaves irregularly around you in a 3 aura. That area is difficult terrain for enemies. Additionally, when an ally enters the area for the first time in a combat round or starts their turn there, they gain a +2 bonus to speed until the end of the turn.","href":"talent/level-5/distortion-temporal/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Distracted","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Insurgent","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have mastered the ability to distract your foes, allowing you and your allies to take advantage of their gaps in attention. Whenever you or any ally attempts to hide, any creature marked by you doesn't count as an observer. Additionally, you and your allies can use other allies as cover for the purpose of hiding.","href":"tactician/level-5/distracted/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Distraction Tactics","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","keywords":["Magic"],"conditions":["taunted"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Special","flavor":"Your minions do the work to draw the heat away from your allies.","href":"ability/summoner/level-1/distraction-tactics/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Dive Bomb","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever the condor makes a strike while rampaging, they deal extra damage equal to the number of squares they've moved on their turn (to a maximum of 5).","href":"companion/beastheart/condor/level-3/dive-bomb/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Divine Comedy","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Trickery","source":"class","level":2,"action":"maneuver","cost":"5 Piety","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"distance":"**5** burst","targets":"Self and each ally in the area","flavor":"You and your allies swap places to confound your foes.","href":"ability/conduit/level-2/divine-comedy/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Divine Dragon","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Creation","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Piety","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Special","flavor":"From nothing but divine will, you create a powerful ally.","href":"ability/conduit/level-9/divine-dragon/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Divine Power","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have an epic resource called divine power. Each time you finish a respite, you gain divine power equal to the XP you gain. You can spend divine power on your abilities as if it were piety.","href":"conduit/level-10/divine-power/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Divine Quartermaster","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Creation","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"Each time you finish a respite, you can choose a treasure with a project goal equal to 50 times your level or less. You gain a divine version of this treasure that lasts until you finish another respite or it is consumed.","href":"censor/level-7/divine-quartermaster/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Divine Quartermaster","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Creation","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"Each time you finish a respite, you can choose a treasure with a project goal equal to 50 times your level or less. You gain a divine version of this treasure that lasts until you finish another respite or it is consumed.","href":"conduit/level-7/divine-quartermaster/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Doctrine Triggered Action","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your tactical doctrine grants you a triggered action, as shown on the Doctrine Triggered Actions table.","options":4,"href":"tactician/level-1/doctrine-triggered-action/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Dogpile","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":5,"action":"main","cost":"9 Ferocity","keywords":["Beastheart","Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["grabbed","prone"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You and your allies surround your enemy like a pack of wolves, mobbing them and pulling them down.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-5/dogpile/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Domain Piety and Effects","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your choice of domains provides you with two additional ways to earn piety during combat, as triggered by specific events. You might even have a single event trigger both your piety effects. For example, the Sun domain grants piety when a nearby creature takes fire or holy damage, while the War domain grants piety when a nearby creature takes damage of 10 + your level or higher. If you have both those domains and a nearby creature takes an appropriate amount of fire damage, you gain piety from both your domains.","options":12,"href":"conduit/level-1/domain-piety-and-effects/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Doomsight","klass":"Hakaan","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Working with your Director, you can predetermine an encounter in which you will die. When that encounter begins, you become doomed. While doomed, you automatically obtain a tier 3 outcome on tests and ability rolls, and you don't die no matter how low your Stamina falls. You then die immediately at the end of the encounter, and can't be returned to life by any means.","href":"trait/hakaan/doomsight/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Doubling the Hours","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Chronopathy","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"While you have 5 or more Victories, you can undertake an additional respite activity during a respite.","href":"talent/level-8/doubling-the-hours/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Doubt","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":8,"action":"main","cost":"11 Clarity","keywords":["Animapathy","Psionic","Ranged","Strike"],"conditions":["slowed","weakened"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"You tug at the strings of the foe's anima and unravel them, allowing someone else to take advantage of their drive.","href":"ability/talent/level-8/doubt/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Draconian Guard","klass":"Dragon Knight","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you or an adjacent creature takes damage from a strike, you can use a triggered action to guard against the blow. Earth","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Earth","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You harness the flow of earth magic to become harder to move. Whenever you use an ability that has the Earth and Magic keywords, your stability increases by 1 until the start of your next turn. This benefit is cumulative.","href":"elementalist/level-1/earth-acolyte-of-earth/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Ease the Hours","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Chronopathy","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can increase the number of rounds in a montage test by 1 if the test would end before the heroes hit the success limit.","href":"talent/level-2/ease-the-hours/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Ease the Mind","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Telepathy","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"You gain an edge on tests made to stop combat and start a negotiation. Additionally, if you are present during a negotiation, any NPC who has a hostile or suspicious starting attitude has their patience increased by 1 (to a maximum of 5).","href":"talent/level-2/ease-the-mind/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Ease Their Fall","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Telekinesis","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you land after a fall, or if any falling creature lands within 2 squares of you, you can use a free triggered action to reduce the falling damage by an amount equal to 2 + your Reason score.","href":"talent/level-2/ease-their-fall/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Edict of Disruptive Isolation","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Wrath","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"distance":"**2** aura","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"The evil within your foes detonates with holy fire that burns only the guilty.","href":"ability/censor/level-3/edict-of-disruptive-isolation/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Edict of Peace","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Oracle","source":"class","level":6,"action":"maneuver","cost":"9 Wrath","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"distance":"**3** aura","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"You anticipate your foes' moves and deny them.","href":"ability/censor/level-6/edict-of-peace/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Edict of Perfect Order","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Wrath","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"distance":"**2** aura","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"Within the area of your divine presence, your enemies will regret using their fell abilities.","href":"ability/censor/level-3/edict-of-perfect-order/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Edict of Purifying Pacifism","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Wrath","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"distance":"**2** aura","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"You shed a righteous energy that punishes enemies who would harm you or your allies.","href":"ability/censor/level-3/edict-of-purifying-pacifism/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Edict of Stillness","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Wrath","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"distance":"**2** aura","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"The holy aura you project makes it painful for evil-doers to leave your reach.","href":"ability/censor/level-3/edict-of-stillness/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Edict of Unyielding Resolve","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Paragon","source":"class","level":9,"action":"maneuver","cost":"11 Wrath","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"distance":"**2** aura","targets":"Self and each ally in the area","flavor":"You and your allies are clad in shimmering armor.","href":"ability/censor/level-9/edict-of-unyielding-resolve/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Eidos","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"You gain an epic resource called eidos. When you take a respite, you gain eidos equal to the XP you gain. You can spend eidos as if it were essence on minions and abilities you have. When you do, you summon up to two bonus signature minions into unoccupied spaces within your Summoner's Range.","href":"summoner/level-10/eidos/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Electric Surge","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"The first time on a turn that you or the spark deal lightning damage, you gain 1 surge.","href":"companion/beastheart/elemental-spark/level-1/electric-surge/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Electroshock","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever the spark makes a strike against a creature while rampaging, they can cause a target who has M \u003c AVERAGE to be dazed until the end of the target's next turn.","href":"companion/beastheart/elemental-spark/level-3/electroshock/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Elemental Absorption","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you use your Inertial Shield triggered action, you gain immunity to acid, cold, corruption, fire, lightning, poison, and sonic damage equal to your Intuition score against the triggering damage.","href":"null/level-6/elemental-absorption/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Elemental Affinity","klass":"Summoner","subclass":"Storms","feature_source":"circle","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you use Call Forth to summon one or more non-signature elemental minions, you can summon one bonus signature minion at no cost. You can choose between a signature minion that shares an Element keyword with the minions you summoned (such as Fire, Earth, or Air) or an elemental mote.","href":"summoner/level-1/elemental-affinity/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Elemental Avatar","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"While the drake is rampaging, you and the drake have immunity all to the drake's attuned damage type, and whenever you or the drake make a strike against a creature you can cause the target to be dragonsealed (save ends). A dragonsealed creature has weakness 10 to the drake's attuned damage type.","href":"companion/beastheart/drake/level-10/elemental-avatar/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Elemental Buffer","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you reduce acid, cold, corruption, fire, lightning, poison, or sonic damage with damage immunity, you gain 2 surges that can be used only to increase the damage of your next strike.","href":"null/level-6/elemental-buffer/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Elemental Ferocity","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":8,"action":"maneuver","cost":"11 Ferocity","keywords":["Magic"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"Your primordial energy makes for instant retribution.","href":"ability/fury/level-8/elemental-ferocity/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Elemental Form","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"You exhibit ever-stronger signs of how the force of the Primordial Chaos flows within you. Whenever you show strong emotion or increase your ferocity, elemental motes attuned to your mood flit around you, and your skin changes in appearance to reflect an element of your choice.","href":"fury/level-7/elemental-form/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Elemental Specialization","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Through your studies, you know and can manipulate the seven primal elements of the timescape:","options":4,"href":"elementalist/level-1/elemental-specialization/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Elementalist Abilities","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Choose two signature abilities from the following options. Signature abilities can be used at will. (Quick Build: Bifurcated Incineration, Viscous Fire.)","options":2,"href":"elementalist/level-1/elementalist-abilities/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Elementalist Ward","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You create an invisible elemental ward that protects you. Choose one of the following wards. You can change your ward and enchantment (see above) by performing a complex ritual as a respite activity. (Quick Build: Ward of Surprising Reactivity.)","options":4,"href":"elementalist/level-1/elementalist-ward/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Elementally Attuned","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"When you gain this companion, you choose their attuned damage type from acid, cold, corruption, fire, lightning, poison, or sonic. The drake's attuned damage type affects their other features.","href":"companion/beastheart/drake/level-1/elementally-attuned/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Elements Unleashed","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Spark","source":"class","level":6,"action":"maneuver","cost":"9 Ferocity","keywords":["Companion","Magic"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"Your companion's body becomes a bank of glowing coals, a web of arcing lightning, a cloud of rumbling thunder, or a flurry of dancing ice crystals.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-6/elements-unleashed/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Elite Formation","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Each of your minions have their Stamina increased by 3 and their stability increased by 1.","href":"summoner/level-1/elite-formation/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Emergency Ward","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"The first time each round you take damage, you can use a free triggered action to shift 1 after the triggering effect resolves and summon a signature minion into the square you left (as long as there is enough space).","href":"summoner/level-3/emergency-ward/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"En Garde!","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Duelist","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Drama","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Wait, it's... Guard! Turn! Parry! Dodge! Spin! Thrust! Ha!","href":"ability/troubadour/level-2/en-garde/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Enchantment","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You weave an elemental enchantment into your body that enhances your statistics. Choose one of the following enchantments. You can change your enchantment and ward (see Elementalist Ward below) by performing a complex ritual as a respite activity. (Quick Build: Enchantment of Destruction.)","options":5,"href":"elementalist/level-1/enchantment/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Enchantment of Battle","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can wear light armor and wield light weapons effectively, even though you don't have a kit. While you wear light armor, you gain a +3 bonus to Stamina, and that bonus increases by 3 at 4th, 7th, and 10th levels. While you wield a light weapon, you gain a +1 damage bonus with weapon abilities, including free strikes. You can use light armor treasures and light weapon treasures.","href":"elementalist/level-1/enchantment-of-battle/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Enchantment of Celerity","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You gain a +1 bonus to speed and to the distance you can shift when you take the Disengage move action.","href":"elementalist/level-1/enchantment-of-celerity/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Enchantment of Destruction","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You gain a +1 bonus to rolled damage with magic abilities.","href":"elementalist/level-1/enchantment-of-destruction/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Enchantment of Distance","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have a +2 bonus to the distance of your ranged magic abilities.","href":"elementalist/level-1/enchantment-of-distance/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Enchantment of Permanence","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You gain a +6 bonus to Stamina, and this bonus increases by 6 at 4th, 7th, and 10th levels. Additionally, you gain a +1 bonus to stability.","href":"elementalist/level-1/enchantment-of-permanence/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Encore","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Virtuoso","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Drama","keywords":["Magic","Strike"],"distance":"Special","targets":"Special","flavor":"Again! Again!","href":"ability/troubadour/level-2/encore/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Endless Breath","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"The drake's Drake Breath maneuver deals an extra 2 damage.","href":"companion/beastheart/drake/level-3/endless-breath/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Enhanced Null Field","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"While using your Null Field ability, you disrupt magic and psionic power suffusing the area around you. During combat, any temporary supernatural terrain effects of your level or lower are removed when your aura partially or fully overlaps with their location. Permanent supernatural terrain effects of your level or lower are temporarily negated while your aura overlaps with their location, but return when the aura no longer overlaps with them.","href":"null/level-4/enhanced-null-field/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Entropic Adaptability","klass":"Null","subclass":"Cryokinetic","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have cold immunity equal to twice your Intuition score. Additionally, you ignore difficult terrain related to cold and ice, and you can automatically climb at full speed while moving.","href":"null/level-2/entropic-adaptability/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Entropic Bolt","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Chronopathy","Psionic","Ranged","Strike"],"conditions":["slowed"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"You advance an enemy's age for a moment.","href":"ability/talent/level-1/entropic-bolt/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Entropic Field","klass":"Null","subclass":"Cryokinetic","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Discipline","keywords":["Area","Psionic","Weapon"],"conditions":["slowed"],"distance":"**3** cube within **1**","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"You drastically increase the local entropy.","href":"ability/null/level-2/entropic-field/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Entropy Ward","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your ward slows time for your enemies. Whenever a creature deals damage to you, their speed is reduced by an amount equal to your Reason score and they can't use triggered actions until the end of their next turn.","href":"talent/level-1/entropy-ward/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Epic","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Auteur","source":"class","level":9,"action":"maneuver","cost":"11 Drama","keywords":["Magic","Melee","Ranged"],"distance":"Melee **1** or ranged **10**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Your story tells a tale of the villain's waning power and how the heroes rose to the occasion to stop them.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-9/epic/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Equal Billing","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can use your Scene Partner feature to form a bond with one willing hero instead of an NPC you interact with using a test. If you bond with another hero, you lose your existing bond with a hero.","href":"troubadour/level-7/equal-billing/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Equipment","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"You wear no armor and wield only your unarmed strikes—which become devastating natural weapons as your ferocity grows.","href":"fury/stormwight-kits/equipment/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Erase","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":3,"action":"main","cost":"7 Essence","keywords":["Magic","Ranged","Strike","Void"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Special","flavor":"With a flick of the wrist, you phase creatures out of existence.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-3/erase/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Escape Grab","klass":"Common","source":"other","level":0,"action":"maneuver","conditions":["grabbed"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","href":"ability/common/escape-grab/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Escape Grab","source":"other","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"A creature who is grabbed by another creature, an object, or an effect (see Grab below) can attempt to escape by using the following ability.","grants":"the Escape Grab maneuver","href":"common/maneuvers/escape-grab/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Essence","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You channel the substance of creation in the form of a Heroic Resource called essence, gathering and burning it to cast and maintain spells.","options":2,"href":"elementalist/level-1/essence/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Essence","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You and your minions have a unique reserve of essence as your Heroic Resource. You use this magic to sculpt your forces and maintain control over the battlefield.","options":2,"href":"summoner/level-1/essence/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Essence Funnel","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":3,"action":"main","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"distance":"**10** × **1** line within **1**","targets":"Each enemy and object in the area","flavor":"You rapidly summon and sacrifice minions in order to power a devastating blast of magic.","href":"ability/summoner/level-3/essence-funnel/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Essence Salvage","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"The first time each combat round that any minion unwillingly dies within your Summoner's Range, you gain 2 essence instead of 1.","href":"summoner/level-4/essence-salvage/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Essence Transfer","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","keywords":["Magic","Melee","Strike"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You pierce your foe and repurpose some of that 'fiber of their being' they weren't using.","href":"ability/summoner/level-1/essence-transfer/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Essential Being","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"When you gain essence at the start of each of your turns during combat, you gain 4 essence instead of 3.","href":"elementalist/level-10/essential-being/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Every Step... Death!","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Magic","Ranged","Strike"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You show your foe a glimpse of their fate after death.","href":"ability/censor/level-1/every-step-death/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Everyone's Best Friend","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your companion may not be much of a talker, but they've got a lifetime of experience surviving the dangers of the wild. They can offer aid in nearly any circumstance: helping exhausted travelers find their way, leading panicked villagers out of a burning building, or even providing a comforting nuzzle at just the right time. Once per round during a montage test, when you or another character makes a test, your companion can increase the tier outcome by one tier (to a maximum of tier 3).","href":"beastheart/level-2/everyones-best-friend/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Everywhere and Nowhere","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"While the lightbender is rampaging, your grip on spatial reality is weakened, and once on each of your turns, you or the lightbender can teleport up to 3 spaces as a free maneuver. Additionally, strikes made against you and the lightbender have a double bane until the lightbender's rampage ends.","href":"companion/beastheart/lightbender/level-10/everywhere-and-nowhere/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Evil Revealed","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Exorcist","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your order has taught you methods to discern the disguises of both mortals and monsters. You automatically see through disguises and illusions created by creatures of your level or lower, and you gain an edge on tests made to see through the disguises and illusions of more powerful creatures. Whenever you see through a creature's disguise or illusion, you can use your Judgment ability against them as a free triggered action.","href":"censor/level-5/evil-revealed/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Eviscerate","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"3 Insight","keywords":["Melee","Ranged","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["bleeding"],"distance":"Melee **1** or ranged **5**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You leave your foe bleeding out after a devastating attack.","href":"ability/shadow/level-1/eviscerate/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Excommunication","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":8,"action":"main","cost":"11 Wrath","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["weakened"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You curse your foe to become a bane to their allies.","href":"ability/censor/level-8/excommunication/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Exothermic Shield","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":5,"action":"maneuver","cost":"9 Clarity","keywords":["Pyrokinesis","Psionic","Ranged"],"conditions":["slowed","weakened"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"You encase the target in psionic flame and allow them to flicker without fear of burning out.","href":"ability/talent/level-5/exothermic-shield/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Expanded Power","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"If the ability targets an area, you increase the size of the area by 1. If the area is a line, you increase the size of one dimension, not both.","href":"null/level-7/expanded-power/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Expanded Power","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"If the ability targets an area, you increase the size of the area by 1. If the area is a line, you increase the size of one dimension, not both.","href":"talent/level-6/expanded-power/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Expert Fencer","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Duelist","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Drama","keywords":["Charge","Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["bleeding"],"distance":"Melee **3**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"If you can land the strike, the crowd goes wild.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-9/expert-fencer/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Explosive Assistance","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Fire","source":"class","level":1,"action":"triggered","keywords":["Fire","Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"You add a little magic to an ally's aggression at just the right time.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-1/explosive-assistance/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Explosive Parade","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Summoner's Range","targets":"Special","flavor":"Your minions swell with energy until they can no longer exist in this realm.","href":"ability/summoner/level-1/explosive-parade/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Extended Power","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"If the ability is ranged, the distance gains a bonus equal to your Intuition score. If the ability is melee, the distance gains a +2 bonus.","href":"null/level-7/extended-power/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Extended Power","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"If the ability is ranged, the distance gains a bonus equal to your Reason score. If the ability is melee, the distance gains a +2 bonus.","href":"talent/level-6/extended-power/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Extensive Rewrites","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Drama","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"distance":"**4** burst","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"No, this isn't right. That foe was over there!","href":"ability/troubadour/level-3/extensive-rewrites/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Face the Storm!","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Ferocity","keywords":["Magic"],"conditions":["taunted"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"Shocked in the face of your naked brutality, your enemy's instincts take over.","href":"ability/fury/level-3/face-the-storm/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Fairy Whispers","klass":"Summoner","subclass":"Spring","feature_source":"circle","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you send a minion to perform a task for you outside of combat, they can bring back a rumor from the destination to which you sent them. You can't be made frightened.","href":"trait/polder/fearless/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Feedback","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Virtuoso","source":"class","level":6,"action":"main","cost":"9 action","href":"talent/level-1/feedback-loop/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Feral Heart","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"At the start of each of your turns in burst","targets":"Each creature in the area","href":"ability/beastheart/level-1/feral-strike/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Ferocity","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You and your companion tap into a predator's bloodlust that grants you a Heroic Resource called ferocity.","options":2,"href":"beastheart/level-1/ferocity/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Ferocity","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Within the heat of battle, your determination and anger grow, fueling a Heroic Resource called ferocity.","options":2,"href":"fury/level-1/ferocity/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Ferox","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have an epic resource called ferox. Each time you finish a respite, you gain ferox equal to the XP you gain. You can spend ferox on your abilities as if it were ferocity.","href":"beastheart/level-10/ferox/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Fester Field","source":"other","level":9,"action":"trait","flavor":"Each non-abyssal enemy that starts their turn within 3 squares of the boil takes 5 corruption damage.","href":"fixture/demon/the-boil/level-9/fester-field/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Fetch!","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Guardian","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 existence, returning with a visitor you were particularly hoping to meet.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-2/fetch/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Field Arsenal","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"For example, if you take the Shining Armor and Sniper kits, you gain the following benefits overall:","href":"tactician/level-1/field-arsenal/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Fight Choreography","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Duelist","source":"class","level":6,"action":"main","cost":"9 corners.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-6/fight-choreography/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Final Evolution","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"When you gain ferocity at the start of each of your turns during combat, you gain 2d3 + 1 ferocity instead of 1d3 + 1.","href":"beastheart/level-10/final-evolution/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Finish
