opening to your ally so they can land a killing blow.","href":"ability/tactician/level-8/finish-them/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Fire Breath","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Companion","Magic","Melee","Ranged"],"distance":"Melee **1** or Ranged **2**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"The hellhound exhales infernal flames.","href":"ability/companion/beastheart/hellhound/level-1/fire-breath/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Fire Up the Night","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Virtuoso","source":"class","level":3,"action":"none","keywords":["Area","Magic","Performance"],"distance":"**5** aura","targets":"Self and each ally in the area","flavor":"Maybe you and I ♪ We can still bring the light!♪","href":"ability/troubadour/level-3/fire-up-the-night/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Fire: Acolyte of Fire","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Fire","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You become an expert at wielding destructive flames. Your abilities that have the Fire and Magic keywords gain a +1 bonus to rolled damage. Your Hurl Element ability (see below) also gains this bonus when you use it to deal fire damage.","href":"elementalist/level-1/fire-acolyte-of-fire/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Fix It in Post","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Auteur","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"Once on each of your turns, you can use a free maneuver to change one condition affecting a creature within distance of your Dramatic Monologue ability. Choose one of the following conditions on the target: bleeding, frightened, prone, slowed, or taunted. You change that condition to another of those conditions, maintaining the duration and origin of the original condition. A target who is no longer prone can stand up.","href":"troubadour/level-5/fix-it-in-post/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Flash Powder","klass":"Summoner","subclass":"Spring","feature_source":"circle","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"Each ally that gains temporary Stamina from your Pixie Dust feature also gains one of the following effects until the end of their next turn (or for 10 minutes if used outside of combat):","href":"summoner/level-5/flash-powder/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Flashback","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","cost":"5 Clarity","keywords":["Chronopathy","Psionic","Ranged"],"conditions":["slowed"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"The target is thrown several seconds back through time and gets to do it all again.","href":"ability/talent/level-1/flashback/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Flight of the Condor","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"While the condor is rampaging, you and the condor gain a +5 bonus to speed.","href":"companion/beastheart/condor/level-10/flight-of-the-condor/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Fling Through Time","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":3,"action":"main","cost":"7 Clarity","keywords":["Chronopathy","Psionic","Ranged","Strike"],"conditions":["weakened"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"You hurl the target through the annals of time, forcing them to witness every moment of their existence all at once.","href":"ability/talent/level-3/fling-through-time/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Flip the Script","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"5 Drama","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"conditions":["slowed"],"distance":"**3** burst","targets":"Self and each ally in the area","flavor":"You try a different take on events, justifying the new locations everyone ended up in.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-1/flip-the-script/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Floodgates Open","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":8,"action":"main","cost":"11 Focus","keywords":["Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Three allies","flavor":"You direct your squad to strike in unison and with devastating effect.","href":"ability/tactician/level-8/floodgates-open/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Flowering Bed","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Green","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"At the end of each of your turns, each ally in the area gains temporary Stamina equal to your Reason score.","href":"elementalist/level-4/flowering-bed/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Flurry of Wings","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Companion","Melee","Weapon"],"conditions":["taunted","weakened"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"I can't draw a bead on them with that infernal bird flapping in my face!","href":"ability/companion/beastheart/condor/level-1/flurry-of-wings/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Focus","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"The ring of steel panics others but brings order to your mind, granting you a Heroic Resource called focus.","options":2,"href":"tactician/level-1/focus/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Focus Fire!","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"triggered","distance":"Summoner's Range","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"You ensure the enemy can't escape the incoming attack.","href":"ability/summoner/level-1/focus-fire/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Focus Fire!","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following triggered action.","href":"summoner/level-1/focus-fire/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Focus on Their Weaknesses","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"The first time each combat round that you or any ally damages a target marked by you, you gain 2 focus instead of 1.","href":"tactician/level-4/focus-on-their-weaknesses/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Focused Wrath","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"When you gain wrath at the start of each of your turns during combat, you gain 3 wrath instead of 2.","href":"censor/level-7/focused-wrath/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Foe Bowling","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Punisher","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Ferocity","keywords":["Charge","Companion","Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["prone"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Your companion sends one enemy tumbling into another, taking them both out.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-2/foe-bowling/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Foe Thresher","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever the bear targets a creature with a strike that doesn't impose forced movement while rampaging, the bear can push the target up to a number of squares equal to the bear's Might score.","href":"companion/beastheart/bear/level-3/foe-thresher/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Foes Forever Frozen","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever the basilisk makes a strike against a creature while rampaging, the target is stoned (save ends).","href":"companion/beastheart/basilisk/level-3/foes-forever-frozen/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Fog of War","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Insurgent","source":"class","level":2,"action":"maneuver","cost":"5 Focus","keywords":["Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Two creatures","flavor":"Your unorthodox strategy causes enemies to lash out in fear, heedless of who they might be attacking.","href":"ability/tactician/level-2/fog-of-war/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Foil","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Duelist","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"At the start of an encounter, choose one creature within your line of effect. You have a double edge on power rolls made against or in competition with that creature. The chosen creature also has a double edge on power rolls made against or in competition with you. If the chosen creature is reduced to 0 Stamina, you can choose a new foil at the start of the next combat round.","href":"troubadour/level-3/foil/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Folly Field","source":"other","level":9,"action":"trait","flavor":"Each non-fey enemy that starts their turn within 3 squares of the pond has a -1 penalty to saving throws and resisting potencies until the start of their next turn.","href":"fixture/fey/glade-pond/level-9/folly-field/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Font of Creation","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"When you gain essence at the start of each of your turns during combat, you gain 3 essence instead of 2.","href":"summoner/level-7/font-of-creation/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Font of Essence","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"The first time each combat round that you or a creature within 10 squares takes damage that isn't untyped or holy damage, you gain 2 essence instead of 1.","href":"elementalist/level-4/font-of-essence/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Font of Grace","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Life","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"Each time you use your My Life for Yours ability, you gain 1 wrath that can be spent only on that ability during the same turn. If you don't use this wrath, it is lost. Additionally, the target of My Life for Yours gains 10 temporary Stamina.","href":"censor/level-7/font-of-grace/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Font of Grace","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Life","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"Each time you use your Healing Grace ability, you gain 1 piety that can be spent only on that ability during the same turn. If you don't use this piety, it is lost. Additionally, you can use your Minor Miracle feature to return a creature to life even if you don't have their remains.","href":"conduit/level-7/font-of-grace/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Font of Wrath","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"3 Piety","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Special","flavor":"A brilliant column of holy light appears on the battlefield, striking out at nearby enemies.","href":"ability/conduit/level-1/font-of-wrath/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"For the Pack!","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Spark","source":"class","level":9,"action":"triggered","cost":"11 Ferocity","keywords":["Area","Companion","Magic"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"They'd tell stories in hushed tones of your companion's last stand—if any of them lived to tell the tale.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-9/for-the-pack/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Force Augmentation","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your damage-dealing psionic abilities gain a +1 bonus to rolled damage.","href":"null/level-1/force-augmentation/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Force Augmentation","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your damage-dealing psionic abilities gain a +1 bonus to rolled damage.","href":"talent/level-1/force-augmentation/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Force of Storms","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Berserker","source":"class","level":6,"action":"main","cost":"9 Ferocity","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You strike an enemy hard enough to be a projectile that knocks a crowd of creatures around.","href":"ability/fury/level-6/force-of-storms/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Force Orbs","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":3,"action":"main","cost":"7 Clarity","keywords":["Psionic","Ranged","Strike","Telekinesis"],"conditions":["weakened"],"distance":"Self; see below","targets":"Self","flavor":"Spheres of solid psionic energy float around you.","href":"ability/talent/level-3/force-orbs/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Force Redirected","klass":"Null","subclass":"Chronokinetic","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Discipline","keywords":["Melee","Psionic","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **3**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"The force of your strike moves your target in a surprising direction.","href":"ability/null/level-2/force-redirected/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Forceful","klass":"Hakaan","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you force move a creature or object, the forced movement distance gains a +1 bonus.","href":"trait/hakaan/forceful/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Foreshadowing","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can ask the Director for two clues regarding an upcoming encounter or negotiation. One of the clues can be false.","href":"troubadour/level-4/foreshadowing/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Foresight","klass":"Time Raider","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your senses extend past mundane obscuration and the veil of the future alike. You automatically know the location of any creature with concealment who isn't hidden from you within 20, and you negate the usual bane on strikes against such creatures. Additionally, whenever you are targeted by a strike, you can use a triggered action to impose a bane on the power roll.","href":"trait/time-raider/foresight/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Forest Walk","klass":"Wode Elf","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can shift into and while within difficult terrain.","href":"trait/wode-elf/forest-walk/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Formal Introductions","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"As a respite activity, you can scribe a notice of your arrival (such as a calling card or a formal letter) addressed to an enemy. You can deliver the notice to the target personally if you are in the same general area, send it by courier, or leave it in a covert location for the target to find. You can have only one notice active at a time.","href":"troubadour/level-2/formal-introductions/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Formation","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You've practiced a specific formation for your minions. Choose one of the following formations: horde, platoon, elite, or leader. You can change your formation along with your quick command (see Quick Command) by performing intense study as a respite activity. (Quick Build: Platoon Formation.)","options":4,"href":"summoner/level-1/formation/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Fortress of Perfect Thought","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":9,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your mind is an impenetrable palace that shields you from danger. You gain the following effects:","href":"talent/level-9/fortress-of-perfect-thought/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Four-Armed Athletics","klass":"Time Raider","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your unique physiology enhances your movement. You gain an edge on tests that use the Climb, Gymnastics, or Swim skills when you can use all your arms in the attempt.","href":"trait/time-raider/four-armed-athletics/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Four-Armed Martial Arts","klass":"Time Raider","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your multiple arms let you take on multiple tasks at the same time. Whenever you use the Grab or Knockback maneuver against an adjacent creature, you can target one additional adjacent creature, using the same power roll for both targets. Additionally, you can have up to two creatures grabbed at a time.","href":"trait/time-raider/four-armed-martial-arts/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Free Strike","source":"other","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"A creature can use this main action to make a free strike (see Free Strikes below). Most of the time, you'll want to use the more impactful main actions granted by your class, kit, or other feature, just as the Director will use the main actions in a creature's stat block, but free strikes are available for when all else fails. For instance, a fury who has no other options for ranged strikes might use the Ranged Weapon Free Strike ability with an improvised weapon when battling a flying foe.","href":"common/main-actions/free-strike/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Friend!","klass":"Shadow","subclass":"Harlequin Mask","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your illusions make your enemies believe you are their friend in critical moments. Whenever an enemy uses an ability or trait that targets multiple allies and you are within distance of the effect, you can choose to be a target of the effect as well.","href":"shadow/level-2/friend/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Frontal Assault","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Focus","distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"The purpose of a charge is to break their morale and force a retreat.","href":"ability/tactician/level-3/frontal-assault/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Fulcrum","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Telekinesis","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Clarity","keywords":["Area","Psionic","Telekinesis"],"distance":"Special","targets":"Each enemy and object in the area","flavor":"You precisely manipulate the creatures around you.","href":"ability/talent/level-9/fulcrum/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Fulfill Your Destiny","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Oracle","source":"class","level":9,"action":"triggered","cost":"11 Wrath","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One ally","flavor":"You have looked at various futures, and only this one works.","href":"ability/censor/level-9/fulfill-your-destiny/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Furious Change","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Stormwight","source":"class","level":1,"action":"triggered","distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"In your anger, you revert to a more bestial form.","href":"ability/fury/level-1/furious-change/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Fury Abilities","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Choose one signature ability from the following options. Signature abilities can be used at will. (Quick Build: To the Death!)","options":2,"href":"fury/level-1/fury-abilities/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Garden of Jest","source":"other","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can spend a Recovery the first time in a round a creature gains or starts their turn with a condition while you have line of effect to the pond. Alternatively, you can choose to enable an ally who also has line of effect to the boil to spend a Recovery instead.","href":"fixture/fey/glade-pond/level-5/garden-of-jest/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Gasping in Pain","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["prone"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Your precise strikes let your allies take advantage of a target's agony.","href":"ability/shadow/level-1/gasping-in-pain/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Gelatinous","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"The ball can occupy another creature's space. While occupying a creature's space, the ball has line of effect to that creature. If the creature completely fits within the ball's space, the creature has line of effect only to the ball and creatures outside the ball don't have line of effect to the creature. The ball's space is difficult terrain.","href":"companion/beastheart/gummy-ball/level-1/gelatinous/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Get In Get Out","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"3 Insight","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Move unexpectedly, strike fast, and be gone!","href":"ability/shadow/level-1/get-in-get-out/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Glamor of Terror","klass":"High Elf","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"When a foe strikes, you reverse the magic of your glamor to instill fear into their heart. Whenever you take damage from a creature, you can use a triggered action to make that creature frightened of you until the end of their next turn.","href":"trait/high-elf/glamor-of-terror/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Gloom Squad","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":9,"action":"trait","flavor":"At the start of each of your turns, you can forgo gaining insight to create 1d6 clones of yourself in unoccupied adjacent spaces. A clone acts on your turn and uses your statistics, except they have 1 Stamina. They are affected by any conditions and effects on you, and last until the start of your next turn. A clone doesn't have insight and can't use the Careful Observation ability, the Umbral Form feature, or any triggered actions. On their turn, a clone has a move action, a maneuver, and a main action that they can use only to make a free strike. While making a free strike, a clone must choose targets that you or another clone aren't also striking.","href":"shadow/level-9/gloom-squad/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Glowing Eyes","klass":"Devil","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your eyes are a solid, vibrant color that flares to show your excitement or rage. Whenever you take damage from a creature, you can use a triggered action to deal that creature psychic damage equal to 1d10 + your level.","href":"trait/devil/glowing-eyes/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Glowing Recovery","klass":"Orc","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your bloodfire allows you to regain your strength quicker than others. Whenever you use the Catch Breath maneuver, you can spend as many Recoveries as you like.","href":"trait/orc/glowing-recovery/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Go Now and Speed Well","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":8,"action":"main","cost":"11 Focus","keywords":["Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"You direct an attack to strike true.","href":"ability/tactician/level-8/go-now-and-speed-well/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Goaded","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Mastermind","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have learned to leverage your marked foes' psychology and goad them into acting before they're tactically ready. Whenever a creature marked by you uses a strike that targets you or any ally within your line of effect, you can use a free triggered action to change one target of the strike to you or another ally within your line of effect. The new target must be within distance of the ability and within line of effect of the creature using it.","href":"tactician/level-2/goaded/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Gods Grant Thee Strength","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":5,"action":"main","cost":"9 Wrath","keywords":["Ranged"],"conditions":["prone"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"You channel divine force for movement that cannot be stopped.","href":"ability/censor/level-5/gods-grant-thee-strength/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Gods' Library","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Knowledge","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can gain access to information you need through prayer, so that you no longer require research materials for crafting and research projects (see Chapter 12: Downtime Projects). Additionally, you add your level to project rolls you make for crafting and research projects. You also have any skills in the lore skill group you don't already have, and you gain a number of skills from any other skill groups equal to the number of skills you had in the lore skill group before you gained this feature.","href":"censor/level-7/gods-library/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Gods' Library","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Knowledge","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can gain access to information you need through prayer, so that you no longer require research materials for crafting and research projects (see Chapter 12: Downtime Projects). Additionally, you add your level to project rolls you make for crafting and research projects. You also have any skills in the lore skill group you don't already have, and you gain a number of skills from any other skill groups equal to the number of skills you had in the lore skill group before you gained this feature.","href":"conduit/level-7/gods-library/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Gods' Machine","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Creation","source":"class","level":6,"action":"main","cost":"9 Piety","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Special","flavor":"You conjure a whirring tank made of blades and metal.","href":"ability/conduit/level-6/gods-machine/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Godstorm","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Storm","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 This has no effect on whether you can be grabbed.","href":"null/level-2/inertial-sink/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Inertial Step","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Melee","Psionic","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"You flit about the battlefield and take an opportunistic strike.","href":"ability/null/level-1/inertial-step/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Inescapable Wrath","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Reaver","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have a bonus to speed equal to your Agility score, and you ignore difficult terrain.","href":"fury/level-2/inescapable-wrath/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Infernal Apparition","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever the hellhound makes a strike against a creature while rampaging, they can cause a target who has P \u003c AVERAGE to be frightened until the end of the target's next turn.","href":"companion/beastheart/hellhound/level-3/infernal-apparition/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Infernal Gavotte","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":3,"action":"main","cost":"7 Drama","keywords":["Area","Magic","Melee","Weapon"],"conditions":["weakened"],"distance":"**3** burst","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"A spicy performance lights a fire under your allies' feet.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-3/infernal-gavotte/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Infiltration Tactics","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Insurgent","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have trained your squad to work together, stay silent, and wait for the opportune time to strike. Your own deeds weigh upon you.","href":"ability/censor/level-2/it-is-justice-you-fear/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"It Is the Soul Which Hears","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Green","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can speak with and understand animals, beasts, and plant creatures, even if they don't share a language with you. Your ability to communicate with these creatures doesn't make them inherently more intelligent, but you can use Reason instead of Presence while making tests to influence them.","href":"elementalist/level-1/it-is-the-soul-which-hears/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"It Was Foretold","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Oracle","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your order has trained you to understand fragments of the visions granted to you by your deity, giving you a momentary advantage in challenging situations. At the start of an encounter, you can take one main action before any other creature and before your first turn. Additionally, whenever the Director calls for a montage test, you can make one free test before the montage begins, which counts as an earned success or failure as usual.","href":"censor/level-2/it-was-foretold/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"It Was Me All Along","klass":"Shadow","subclass":"Harlequin Mask","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Insight","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"After everything you've been through together, you twist the blade and make the pain extra personal.","href":"ability/shadow/level-9/it-was-me-all-along/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Jam Session","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Virtuoso","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Drama","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"distance":"**5** burst","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"Your jam session creates new genres that compel everyone to get up and move.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-9/jam-session/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Jaws of This light shines through any darkness. Hidden creatures in the area are automatically revealed, and creatures in the light, including you, can't hide. While this feature is active, you gain an edge on tests made to notice hidden objects and entrances and to detect supernatural illusions.","href":"censor/level-4/light-of-revelation/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Light of the Burning Sun","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Sun","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"Sun infuses your body. Additionally, you have fire immunity equal to your level, which is added to any other fire immunity you have.","href":"conduit/level-7/light-of-the-burning-sun/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Lightbearer","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"While the lightbender is rampaging, you can use a free maneuver to glow with blinding light that lasts until the rampage ends or you use this ability again. Additionally, whenever a creature judged by you is reduced to 0 Stamina and you use Judgment as a free triggered action, if the new target has P \u003c STRONG, they are frightened of you (save ends). If the target is already frightened of you, they instead take holy damage equal to twice your Presence score.","href":"censor/level-3/look-on-my-work-and-despair/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Look!","klass":"Shadow","subclass":"Harlequin Mask","source":"class","level":6,"action":"maneuver","cost":"9 Insight","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"distance":"**5** burst","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"You distract your foes, allowing your allies to take advantage of that distraction.","href":"ability/shadow/level-6/look/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Love Song","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":5,"action":"maneuver","cost":"9 Drama","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"You play a small ditty that plants you inside your target's heart.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-5/love-song/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Low Gravity","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Telekinesis","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your mind can carry your body through tough times. score.","href":"null/level-7/magnified-power/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Magnified Power","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"If the ability has a potency, you maneuvers if made in combat. Searching a chest with a Reason test, picking a door's lock with an Agility test, or lifting a portcullis with a Might test would all be maneuvers. Assisting a test is also a maneuver in combat (see Assist a Test in Chapter 9: Tests).","href":"common/maneuvers/make-or-assist-a-test/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Make Peace With Your God!","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","cost":"5 Ferocity","distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"Anger is your energy.","href":"ability/fury/level-1/make-peace-with-your-god/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Manifold Body","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"When you gain discipline at the start of each of your turns during combat, you gain 4 discipline instead of 3.","href":"null/level-10/manifold-body/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Manifold Resonance","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your body becomes perfected matter, beyond the whims and chaos of the timescape and the restrictions of the manifolds. Each time you finish a respite, you can shift yourself and any creatures in the area of your Null Field ability to any location in the timescape known to you, known to any other creature in the area, or where any supernatural treasure in the area has been before.","href":"null/level-10/manifold-resonance/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Mantle of Essence","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"While you have 3 or more essence and are not dying, you exude an aura of magic whose distance is equal to your Reason score. The effects within the area of the aura are based on your specialization, as shown on the Mantle of Essence Specialization Effects table. You can activate and deactivate the aura at will (no action required).","options":5,"href":"elementalist/level-4/mantle-of-essence/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Mantle of Quintessence","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your Mantle of Essence feature no longer requires essence.","href":"elementalist/level-7/mantle-of-quintessence/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Marauder of the Primordial Chaos","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Stormwight","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"As your connection to the power of the Primordial Chaos grows ever stronger, you automatically sense any elemental creatures or magic sources of elemental power, such as a lava pool or a lake overlapping with Quintessence, within 1 mile of you.","href":"fury/level-6/marauder-of-the-primordial-chaos/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Mark","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","keywords":["Ranged"],"conditions":["taunted"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You draw your allies' attention to a specific foe—with devastating effect.","href":"ability/tactician/level-1/mark/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Mark","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You know how to focus the attention of your allies as you push them toward victory. You have the following ability.","grants":"the Mark maneuver","href":"tactician/level-1/mark/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Martial Artist (Battle Grace)","klass":"Martial Artist","source":"kit","level":0,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You feint to move your enemies into perfect position.","href":"ability/Kits/martial-artist-battle-grace/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Master of Arms","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your expertise with weapons has grown to true mastery. Whenever you use a signature ability from one of your equipped kits or make a free strike using a weapon from one of your equipped kits, you can negate a bane on the power roll or reduce a double bane to a bane.","href":"tactician/level-6/master-of-arms/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Master of Earth","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Earth","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have damage immunity 5.","href":"elementalist/level-10/master-of-earth/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Master of Fire","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Fire","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"The damage bonus of your Acolyte of Fire feature increases to +5 and applies to all your magic abilities.","href":"elementalist/level-10/master-of-fire/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Master of Green","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Green","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"The number of Recoveries you have increases by 2, and each time you finish a respite, you can grant each ally who finished the respite with you 2 additional Recoveries. Your allies' additional Recoveries disappear when they finish their next respite.","href":"elementalist/level-10/master-of-green/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Master of Void","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Void","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you willingly move, you can teleport.","href":"elementalist/level-10/master-of-void/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Masterwork","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Duelist","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"Choose one of your signature abilities and name it after yourself. You always have this ability available, even if it is sourced from a kit you switch out. Whenever you use this ability, you gain an edge and 1 surge that you can use only on this ability.","href":"troubadour/level-8/masterwork/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Materialize","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Psionic","Ranged","Resopathy","Strike"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"You picture an object in your mind and give it form—directly above your opponent's head.","href":"ability/talent/level-1/materialize/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Maw of Earth","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":3,"action":"main","cost":"7 Essence","keywords":["Area","Earth","Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"**3** cube within **10**","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"You open up the ground, spewing out shrapnel of stone and debris.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-3/maw-of-earth/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Medley","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Virtuoso","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can maintain two performances at a time using your Routines feature.","href":"troubadour/level-5/medley/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Melee Superiority","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Vanguard","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"After constant drills, you can more accurately anticipate an enemy's plan and thwart their attempts to move across the battlefield. Whenever you make an opportunity attack, the target's speed is reduced to 0 until the end of the current turn.","href":"tactician/level-2/melee-superiority/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Melee Weapon Free Strike","klass":"Common","source":"other","level":0,"action":"main","keywords":["Charge","Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature or object","href":"ability/common/melee-weapon-free-strike/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Melodrama","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have more ways of getting the most drama out of a situation. Choose two of the following events to add to the events that grant you drama during battle:","href":"troubadour/level-4/melodrama/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Melt Away","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Prowler","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"You or your companion can use your Shadow in the Mist ability even when targeted by the triggering ability.","href":"beastheart/level-5/melt-away/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Melt Their Faces","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Virtuoso","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Drama","keywords":["Magic","Melee","Ranged","Strike"],"distance":"Melee **1** or ranged **10**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"The power of music rips through the reality around the target and blows them away.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-9/melt-their-faces/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Memento Mori","source":"other","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"You gain a surge the first time in a round one of your undead minions unwillingly dies while you have line of effect to the gates. You can choose to give the surge to an ally who also has line of effect to the gates.","href":"fixture/undead/barrow-gates/level-5/memento-mori/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Memonek Traits","klass":"Memonek","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Memonek heroes have access to the following traits.","options":3,"href":"trait/memonek/memonek-traits/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Menagerie","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Stormwight","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can use all stormwight kits. During a respite, you can choose to swap your stormwight kit and still take another respite activity. Your Nature's Knight feature now lets you automatically sense the presence of animals within 1 mile of you. Additionally, whenever you make a test to track another creature, you can roll three dice and choose which two to use.","href":"fury/level-8/menagerie/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Metakinetic Mastery","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you use your Inertial Shield ability, you can then use the Knockback maneuver as a free triggered action.","options":1,"href":"null/level-1/metakinetic-mastery/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Meteor","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":6,"action":"main","cost":"9 Essence","keywords":["Earth","Fire","Magic","Ranged","Void"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"You teleport the target into the air and let the ground and the elemental force of fire do the rest.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-6/meteor/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Meteoric Introduction","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Earth","Magic","Melee","Strike"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"You give your enemy a gentle tap-like an asteroid impact.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-1/meteoric-introduction/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Method Acting","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"5 Drama","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["bleeding","weakened"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"They're so hurt by your performance, you start to believe it yourself.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-1/method-acting/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Mighty Leaps","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can't obtain lower than a tier 2 outcome on any Might test made to jump (see Movement Types in Chapter 10: Combat).","href":"fury/level-1/mighty-leaps/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Mighty Spring","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever the panther takes the Advance move action or the Charge action, they can jump up to a number of squares equal to their speed in any direction, including vertically, as part of this movement.","href":"companion/beastheart/panther/level-1/mighty-spring/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Mind Game","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"5 Focus","keywords":["Melee","Ranged","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["weakened"],"distance":"Melee **1** or ranged **5**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"Your attack demoralizes your foe. Your allies begin to think you can win.","href":"ability/tactician/level-1/mind-game/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Mind Projection","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"As a maneuver, you project your mind outside your body. While you are in this state, your body remains unconscious and prone, and your mind is a separate entity with size 1T. Your mind automatically has concealment, and can freely move through solid matter. If you end your turn inside solid matter, you are forced out into the space where you entered it.","href":"talent/level-4/mind-projection/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Mind Recovery","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you spend a Recovery to regain Stamina while strained, you can forgo the Stamina and gain 3 clarity instead.","href":"talent/level-4/mind-recovery/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Mind Snare","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":5,"action":"main","cost":"9 Clarity","keywords":["Psionic","Ranged","Strike","Telepathy"],"conditions":["slowed"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You latch onto your prey's brain and don't let go, like a song they can't get out of their head.","href":"ability/talent/level-5/mind-snare/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Mind Spike","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","keywords":["Psionic","Ranged","Strike","Telepathy"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"A telepathic bolt instantly zaps a creature's brain.","href":"ability/talent/level-1/mind-spike/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Mind Spike","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following ability, which can be used as a ranged free strike.","grants":"the Mind Spike main action","href":"talent/level-1/mind-spike/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Mindlink","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Telepathy","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"During a respite, you can choose a number of creatures up to your Reason score who you have communicated with using your Telepathic Speech feature, creating a telepathic link among all of you. Whenever a linked creature spends one or more Recoveries, each other linked creature can spend a Recovery.","href":"talent/level-8/mindlink/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Mindwipe","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":8,"action":"main","cost":"11 Clarity","keywords":["Melee","Psionic","Strike","Telepathy"],"distance":"Melee **2**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You attempt to make them forget all their training.","href":"ability/talent/level-8/mindwipe/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Minion Bridge","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","keywords":["Magic"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One of your minions","flavor":"Your minions do everything in their power to form a safe path for you to cross.","href":"ability/summoner/level-1/minion-bridge/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Minion Bridge","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following maneuver.","grants":"the Minion Bridge maneuver","href":"summoner/level-1/minion-bridge/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Minion Chain","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you use Minion Bridge as a maneuver, each of your minions within your Summoner's minions.","href":"summoner/level-4/minion-chain/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Minion Improvement","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"You now start encounters and round-tracked situations by summoning up to two additional minions for every two Victories you have (in addition to the two you normally summon).","options":1,"href":"summoner/level-10/minion-improvement/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Minion Improvement","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your maximum number of minions increases by 4.","options":1,"href":"summoner/level-4/minion-improvement/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Minion Improvement","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"At the start of each of your turns during combat, you can summon one additional signature minion at no cost into an unoccupied space within your Summoner's Range (no action required).","options":1,"href":"summoner/level-7/minion-improvement/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Minion Machinations","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your maximum number of followers increases by 2.","href":"summoner/level-6/minion-machinations/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Minions","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"The creatures you control are called minions. You can summon and maintain up to a maximum of 8 minions. Your minions are considered allies at your level.","options":2,"href":"summoner/level-1/minions/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Minor Acceleration","klass":"Time Raider","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"maneuver","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Psionic","Melee"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"You fill yourself or an ally with a burst of speed.","href":"ability/time-raider/minor-acceleration/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Minor Miracle","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"As a respite activity, you can perform a religious ritual and beseech the gods to restore a dead creature to life. You must have at least half the creature's remains, and they must have died within the last 24 hours from an effect that isn't age related. The creature's soul must be willing to return to life for the ritual to work. If they are not willing, you instinctively understand that as you start the respite activity and can cease it immediately.","href":"conduit/level-3/minor-miracle/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Minor Telekinesis","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Telekinesis","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","keywords":["Psionic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self or one size 1 creature or object","flavor":"Wisps of psychic energy ripple visibly from your brain as you force the target to move using only your mind.","href":"ability/talent/level-1/minor-telekinesis/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Minor Telekinesis","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Telekinesis","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following ability.","grants":"the Minor Telekinesis maneuver","href":"talent/level-1/minor-telekinesis/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Misdirecting Strike","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":3,"action":"main","cost":"7 Insight","keywords":["Melee","Ranged","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["taunted"],"distance":"Melee **1** or ranged **5**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Why are you looking at ME?!","href":"ability/shadow/level-3/misdirecting-strike/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Missed Cue","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Auteur","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"If you aren't surprised at the start of an encounter, you can choose one enemy within your line of effect who is not a leader or solo creature. The Director temporarily removes the chosen creature from the encounter. The chosen creature enters the encounter at the start of the second combat round. You must earn 3 Victories before you can use this feature again.","href":"troubadour/level-3/missed-cue/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Molecular Rearrangement Field","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Discipline","keywords":["Psionic"],"conditions":["bleeding"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"Your enemies' wounds open, your allies' wounds close.","href":"ability/null/level-3/molecular-rearrangement-field/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Moonlight Sonata","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Virtuoso","source":"class","level":8,"action":"none","keywords":["Area","Magic","Performance"],"distance":"**5** aura","targets":"Each ally in the area","flavor":"Music pours out of your heart, filling the area with the utmost delicacy and without damper.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-8/moonlight-sonata/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Morning Light","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Sun","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Piety","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"distance":"**3** burst","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"Light shines at your command, burning your foes and blessing your allies.","href":"ability/conduit/level-2/morning-light/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Most Pious","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"When you roll for piety at the start of your turn in combat and you pray, you gain 1 additional piety.","href":"conduit/level-10/most-pious/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Motivate Earth","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Earth","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","keywords":["Earth","Magic","Melee"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"Special","flavor":"The earth rises, falls, or opens up at your command.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-1/motivate-earth/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Motivate Earth","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Earth","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following ability.","grants":"the Motivate Earth main action","href":"elementalist/level-1/motivate-earth/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Mountain (Pain for tagged you will pay for that.","href":"ability/Kits/mountain-pain-for-pain/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Moving Target","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"While the condor is flying and has a speed greater than 0, ranged strikes against them take a bane.","href":"companion/beastheart/condor/level-1/moving-target/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Muse of Fire","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":8,"action":"main","cost":"11 Essence","keywords":["Area","Fire","Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"**5** cube within **10**","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"The fire burns hot enough to sear the face of any god watching.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-8/muse-of-fire/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"My Life for Yours","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":1,"action":"triggered","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"conditions":["prone"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"You channel some of your vitality into more resilience for you or an ally.","href":"ability/censor/level-1/my-life-for-yours/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"My Life for Yours","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You channel your will to mend your wounds or the wounds of your allies. You have the following ability.","grants":"the My Life for Yours triggered action","href":"censor/level-1/my-life-for-yours/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"My Reputation Precedes Me","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can invoke your reputation at the start of a social interaction with one or more NPCs who haven't met you before, automatically creating a bond with one of those NPCs from that group as if using your Scene Partner feature (above). This bond counts against the limit on active bonds from your Scene Partner feature. While the bond is active, all heroes present treat their Renown as 2 higher than usual for the purpose of entering into a negotiation with the bonded NPC.","href":"troubadour/level-2/my-reputation-precedes-me/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"My Turn!","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":5,"action":"triggered","cost":"9 Ferocity","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"The triggering creature","flavor":"You quickly strike back at a foe.","href":"ability/fury/level-5/my-turn/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"My, What Big Teeth You Have","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever the wolf makes a strike against a creature while rampaging, they can grab the target.","href":"companion/beastheart/wolf/level-3/my-what-big-teeth-you-have/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Nature Judges Thee","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Nature","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Piety","keywords":["Area","Magic","Ranged"],"conditions":["restrained"],"distance":"**3** cube within **10**","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"Mystical thorned vines appear at your bidding and bind your foes.","href":"ability/conduit/level-2/nature-judges-thee/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Nature Watch","klass":"Summoner","subclass":"Storms","feature_source":"circle","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can spend 1 uninterrupted minute each day to perform a ritual and summon a special elemental mote called a beacon to patrol the area. This mote telepathically communicates any hostile creatures, hazards, or traps within 20 squares of them to you no matter how far away you are. You know the number of nearby hazards and which direction they're in relative to where the beacon is, but not their exact position. You can have a number of beacons active equal to your level.","href":"summoner/level-5/nature-watch/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Nature Will Not Harm Us","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Spark","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"You and your companion have damage immunity 10 to cold, fire, lightning, and sonic damage.","href":"beastheart/level-8/nature-will-not-harm-us/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Nature's Bounty","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Nature","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"When you finish a respite, you can prepare a magic meal using local flora for any companions who rested with you. Choose two of the following benefits for creatures who consume the meal:","href":"censor/level-7/natures-bounty/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Nature's Bounty","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Nature","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"When you finish a respite, you can prepare a magic meal using local flora for any companions who rested with you. Choose two of the following benefits for creatures who consume the meal:","href":"conduit/level-7/natures-bounty/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Nature's Knight","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Stormwight","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can speak with animals and elementals. Additionally, you automatically sense the presence of animals and elementals within 10 squares of you, even if they are hidden.","href":"fury/level-3/natures-knight/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Never-Ending Hero","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Virtuoso","source":"class","level":3,"action":"none","keywords":["Area","Magic","Performance"],"conditions":["bleeding"],"distance":"**5** aura","targets":"Self and each ally in the area","flavor":"And toniiight we can truly say ♪ They will alllways find a way!♪","href":"ability/troubadour/level-3/never-ending-hero/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"New 11-Essence Ability","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":9,"action":"trait","flavor":"Choose one heroic ability from the following options, each of which costs 11 essence to use. Alternatively, you can choose one of the 5-essence abilities you didn't select at 1st level (see 1st-Level Features).","options":4,"href":"elementalist/level-2/new-5-essence-ability/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"New 9-Essence Ability","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"Choose one heroic ability from the following options, each of which costs 9 essence to use. (Quick Build: Faster Than the Eye, Inertial Step.)","options":2,"href":"null/level-1/null-abilities/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Null Field","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","keywords":["Area","Psionic"],"distance":"**1** aura","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"You project an aura that dampens the power of your foes.","href":"ability/null/level-1/null-field/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Null Field","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You project a psionic field of order around your body, dampening the effects of supernatural abilities harmful to you and your allies.","grants":"the Null Field maneuver","href":"null/level-1/null-field/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Null Speed","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"The flow of psionic power through you allows you to achieve high velocity. As you shape the growth of your power, you choose a null tradition from the following options, each of which grants you a skill. (Quick Build: Chronokinetic and the Monsters skill.)","href":"null/level-1/null-tradition/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"O Flower Aid, O Earth Defend","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":2,"action":"maneuver","cost":"5 Essence","keywords":["Area","Earth","Green","Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"**3** cube within **10**","targets":"Special","flavor":"Revitalizing plants and jagged stones grow, helping allies and hindering foes.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-2/o-flower-aid-o-earth-defend/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Omnisensory","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have a +10 bonus to the distance of your ranged abilities. Bad boy!","href":"ability/beastheart/level-2/omnomnom/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"On the Razor's Edge","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":8,"action":"main","cost":"11 Ferocity","keywords":["Beastheart","Melee","Ranged","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["bleeding"],"distance":"Melee **1** or ranged **5**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"Driven by the pain and desperation of battle, you and your companion spend your last strength in a flurry of wild attacks.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-8/on-the-razors-edge/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"On You Like Your Shadow","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Prowler","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Ferocity","keywords":["Charge","Companion","Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"Your companion darts around their target, staying out of reach and using them as a shield.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-2/on-you-like-your-shadow/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"One","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"You become the embodiment of the element of your chosen specialization. Whenever you use magic, elemental motes flit around you and your skin changes to reflect your element, taking on an earthen or stony appearance for earth, appearing like flickering flame for fire, gaining a leaf pattern for green, and becoming a starry expanse for void. Additionally, you gain one of the following benefits, as shown on the One Specialization Features table.","options":5,"href":"elementalist/level-10/one/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"One Hundred Visions","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Fate","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your deity rewards you with hazy visions of things to come. Each time you earn 1 or more Victories, you earn an equal number of fate points. Whenever you or a creature within 10 squares makes a test, you can spend 1 fate point to tap into a vision of the outcome, granting that creature an edge on the test. You lose any remaining fate points when you finish a respite.","href":"censor/level-1/oracular-visions/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Oracular Visions","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Fate","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your deity rewards you with hazy visions of things to come. Each time you earn 1 or more Victories, you earn an equal number of fate points. You lose any remaining fate points when you finish a respite.","href":"conduit/level-1/oracular-visions/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Oracular Warning","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Fate","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"Each time you finish a respite, you can share the vague dreams of the future granted to you by the gods with allies who finished the respite with you. These premonitions help you and your allies stay alive, granting each of you temporary Stamina equal to 10 + your level that lasts until you finish another respite.","href":"censor/level-4/oracular-warning/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Orc Traits","klass":"Orc","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Orc heroes have access to the following traits.","options":2,"href":"trait/orc/orc-traits/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Ordained","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":9,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your god elevates the power flowing through you. Your characteristic scores are treated as 1 higher for the purpose of resisting potencies. Additionally, while you have 5 or more Victories, you speak with the voice of your deity. You have a double edge on Presence tests made to influence other creatures.","href":"conduit/level-9/ordained/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Order","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have an epic resource called order. Each time you finish a respite, you gain order equal to the XP you gain. You can then slide the marked target up to 3 squares, ignoring stability. The target can't be moved in a way that would harm them (such as over a cliff), leave them dying, or result in them suffering a condition or other negative effect.","href":"tactician/level-3/out-of-position/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Out of the Way!","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"3
