You gain an edge on tests made using the Endurance skill. Additionally, when you are slowed, your speed is reduced to 3 instead of 2.","href":"trait/human/perseverance/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Persistent Magic","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Some of your heroic abilities have a persistent effect entry. For example, the Instantaneous Excavation ability has an effect noted as \"Persistent 1.\" Whenever you use a persistent ability, you decide whether you want to maintain it, and start doing so immediately after you first use the ability. If you maintain a persistent ability in combat, you reduce the amount of essence you earn at the start of your turn by an amount equal to the ability's persistent value, which enables the ability's persistent effect. All your active persistent abilities end at the end of the encounter.","href":"elementalist/level-1/persistent-magic/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Petrify","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Companion","Magic","Melee","Ranged","Weapon"],"conditions":["slowed"],"distance":"Melee **1** or ranged **5**","targets":"One enemy","flavor":"Transfixed by the basilisk's magical gaze or struck by their poisoned claws, the foe's body begins to calcify.","href":"ability/companion/beastheart/basilisk/level-1/petrify/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Phalanx-Breaker","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Ferocity","keywords":["Melee","Weapon"],"conditions":["dazed"],"distance":"Self; see below","targets":"Self","flavor":"Organizing your forces like feckless creatures of Law. Pitiful.","href":"ability/fury/level-2/phalanx-breaker/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Phase Hurl","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":8,"action":"main","cost":"11 Discipline","keywords":["Melee","Psionic","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["dazed"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You throw your foe out of phase with this manifold, causing them to harm other enemies as they return.","href":"ability/null/level-8/phase-hurl/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Phase Inversion Strike","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Melee","Psionic","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"You step momentarily out of phase as you pull an enemy through you.","href":"ability/null/level-1/phase-inversion-strike/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Phase Leap","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":5,"action":"move","cost":"9 Discipline","keywords":["Psionic"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"You leap beyond reality, leaving an afterimage of yourself.","href":"ability/null/level-5/phase-leap/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Phase Step","klass":"Null","subclass":"Chronokinetic","source":"class","level":6,"action":"main","cost":"9 Discipline","keywords":["Melee","Psionic","Weapon"],"conditions":["dazed"],"distance":"Self; see below","targets":"Self","flavor":"You weaken your connection to this manifold, allowing you to move through and damage enemies.","href":"ability/null/level-6/phase-step/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Phase Strike","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"5 Discipline","keywords":["Melee","Psionic","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["slowed"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"For a moment, your foe slips out of phase with this manifold.","href":"ability/null/level-1/phase-strike/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Pierce the Veil of Substance","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Void","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"Solidity is merely a suggestion to you. Mundane barriers that are 1 square thick or less do not block your senses or line of effect. You can only sense or have line of effect past one such barrier at a time.","href":"elementalist/level-5/pierce-the-veil-of-substance/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Piety","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Additionally, you can gain more piety by praying to the gods—but beware! Doing so can easily draw their ire, as the gods hate to be annoyed. Before you roll to gain piety at the start of your turn, you can pray (no action required). If you do, your roll gains the following additional effects:","options":3,"href":"conduit/level-1/piety/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Pillar of Holy Fire","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":8,"action":"main","cost":"11 Wrath","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["dazed"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Your enemy's guilt fuels a holy flame that burns your foes.","href":"ability/censor/level-8/pillar-of-holy-fire/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Pincer Movement","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Mastermind","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have trained your squad to coordinate their movements to maximize combat impact.","href":"tactician/level-8/pincer-movement/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Pinning Shot","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":3,"action":"main","cost":"7 Insight","keywords":["Ranged","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["restrained"],"distance":"Ranged **5**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"One missile—placed well and placed hard.","href":"ability/shadow/level-3/pinning-shot/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Pixie Dust","klass":"Summoner","subclass":"Spring","feature_source":"circle","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Increase your number of Recoveries by 2.","href":"summoner/level-1/pixie-dust/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Pixie Lift","klass":"Summoner","subclass":"Spring","feature_source":"circle","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your speed gains the Fly and Hover keywords. You lose the Hover keyword from this feature while you are dazed, dying, or you fly more than 1 square above the surface of the ground.","href":"summoner/level-5/pixie-lift/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Plant Walk","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"Once on each of your turns while the sporeling is rampaging, you can teleport to a space within 15 squares as a free maneuver, provided the space or an adjacent space contains the sporeling or plants or fungus of size 1S or larger. You then gain an edge on the next strike you make before the end of your turn.","href":"companion/beastheart/sporeling/level-6/plant-walk/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Platoon Formation","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever one of your squads uses a damaging ability, choose one target of that ability to take additional damage equal to your Reason score.","href":"summoner/level-1/platoon-formation/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Polder Geist","klass":"Polder","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Evading others' notice gives you freedom to move. At the start of each of your turns during combat, if no enemy has line of effect to you or if you are hidden from or have concealment from any enemy with line of effect to you, you gain a +3 bonus to speed until the end of your turn.","href":"trait/polder/polder-geist/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Polder Traits","klass":"Polder","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Polder heroes have access to the following traits.","options":3,"href":"trait/polder/polder-traits/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Portfolio","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"circle","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","href":"summoner/level-1/portfolio/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Portfolio Champion","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"circle","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","href":"summoner/level-8/portfolio-champion/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Pounce","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Companion","Melee","Weapon"],"conditions":["prone"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One enemy","flavor":"The panther bunches up, then uncoils into a deadly leap.","href":"ability/companion/beastheart/panther/level-1/pounce/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Pounce","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Stormwight","source":"class","level":6,"action":"main","cost":"9 Ferocity","keywords":["Magic","Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["grabbed"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You strike at the target like the ultimate predator you are.","href":"ability/fury/level-6/pounce/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Power Ballad","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":8,"action":"maneuver","cost":"11 Drama","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"A song for the brokenhearted wraps itself around the target and blossoms into a ward of thorns.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-8/power-ballad/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Power Chord","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Virtuoso","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"distance":"**2** burst","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"Your instrument rings true and your music blows everyone away.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-1/power-chord/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Power Chord","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Virtuoso","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following ability.","grants":"the Power Chord maneuver","href":"troubadour/level-1/power-chord/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Practical Magic","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","keywords":["Magic"],"distance":"Self; see below","targets":"Self","flavor":"Your mastery of elemental power lets you customize your conjurations.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-1/practical-magic/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Practical Magic","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following ability.","grants":"the Practical Magic maneuver","href":"elementalist/level-1/practical-magic/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Prayer","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your god answers a prayer with enhancements to your body and mind. Choose one of the following prayers. You can change your prayer along with your ward (see Conduit Ward below) by praying to your god as a respite activity. (Quick Build: Prayer of Distance.)","options":5,"href":"conduit/level-1/prayer/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Prayer of Destruction","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your god infuses wrath within your being. You gain a +1 bonus to rolled damage with magic abilities.","href":"conduit/level-1/prayer-of-destruction/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Prayer of Distance","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your god blesses you with the ability to stretch your divine magic farther. You have a +2 bonus to the distance of your ranged magic abilities.","href":"conduit/level-1/prayer-of-distance/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Prayer of Soldier's Skill","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your god gives your mind the training of a soldier. You can wear light armor and wield light weapons effectively, even though you don't have a kit. While you wear light armor, you gain a +3 bonus to Stamina, and that bonus increases by 3 at 4th, 7th, and 10th levels. While you wield a light weapon, you gain a +1 damage bonus with weapon abilities, including free strikes. You can use light armor treasures and light weapon treasures.","href":"conduit/level-1/prayer-of-soldiers-skill/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Prayer of Speed","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your god blesses your flesh and infuses it with divine quickness. You gain a +1 bonus to speed and to the distance you can shift when you take the Disengage move action.","href":"conduit/level-1/prayer-of-speed/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Prayer of Steel","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your god fills your body with the light of creation, making you harder to hurt and move. You gain a +6 bonus to Stamina, and this bonus increases by 6 at 4th, 7th, and 10th levels. Additionally, you gain a +1 bonus to stability.","href":"conduit/level-1/prayer-of-steel/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Precognition","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"3 Clarity","keywords":["Chronopathy","Melee","Psionic"],"distance":"Melee **2**","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"You give a target a glimpse into the future so that they're ready for what comes next.","href":"ability/talent/level-1/precognition/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Prehensile Tail","klass":"Devil","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your prehensile tail allows you to challenge foes on all sides. You can't be flanked.","href":"trait/devil/prehensile-tail/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Prescient Grace","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Oracle","source":"class","level":2,"action":"triggered","cost":"5 Wrath","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"Gifted by a prescient vision, you warn an ally of an impending attack.","href":"ability/censor/level-2/prescient-grace/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Pressure Points","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Melee","Psionic","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["weakened"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"You strike at key nerve clusters to leave your foe staggered.","href":"ability/null/level-1/pressure-points/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Previous Life: 1 Point","klass":"Revenant","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"You select a purchased trait that costs 1 ancestry point from your previous ancestry. You can take this trait multiple times, selecting a different 1 point trait from your previous ancestry each time.","href":"trait/revenant/previous-life-1-point/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Previous Life: 2 Points","klass":"Revenant","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"You select a purchased trait that costs 2 ancestry points from your previous ancestry.","href":"trait/revenant/previous-life-2-points/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Primordial Aspect","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You are a product of customs older than warfare, older than civilization, older than most of the world. You have undergone a rite of passage that revealed the building blocks of the timescape—the Primordial Chaos—and that left an aspect of that chaos inside you. You choose a primordial aspect from the following options, each of which grants you a skill. (Quick Build: Berserker.)","href":"fury/level-1/primordial-aspect/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Primordial Attunement","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"As your ferocity manifests elemental forces created by the Primordial Chaos, you are aware of how elemental power interacts with those around you. You automatically sense whether any creature within 10 squares has damage immunity or damage weakness to acid, cold, corruption, fire, lightning, poison, or sonic damage, learning whether they have immunity or weakness, the value of that immunity or weakness, and the specific damage type. Additionally, you automatically sense any source of one of those damage types within 10 squares, such as a fire or a source of elemental power.","href":"fury/level-4/primordial-attunement/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Primordial Bane","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Reaver","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Ferocity","keywords":["Magic","Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You attune the target to be weaker to a specific element.","href":"ability/fury/level-9/primordial-bane/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Primordial Cunning","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Reaver","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You are never surprised. Additionally, whenever you would push a target with forced movement, you can slide them instead.","href":"fury/level-1/primordial-cunning/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Primordial Ferocity","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"The first time you take damage each combat round, you gain 3 ferocity instead of 2.","href":"fury/level-10/primordial-ferocity/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Primordial Portal","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"As a main action, you can touch a magic source of elemental power and use it to create a portal to Quintessence. You can then use a main action to teleport yourself and any willing creatures within 10 squares of you through the portal and onto a safe island in Quintessence, or to teleport back again. You can maintain a number of portals equal to your Might score, each leading to the same safe island in Quintessence. If a portal in your network is destroyed, it is no longer part of the network. You can remove a portal from your network no matter your distance from it, including across different worlds (no action required).","href":"fury/level-6/primordial-portal/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Primordial Power","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have an epic resource called primordial power. Each time you finish a respite, you gain primordial power equal to the XP you gain. You can spend primordial power on your abilities as if it were ferocity.","href":"fury/level-10/primordial-power/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Primordial Rage","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":8,"action":"maneuver","cost":"11 Ferocity","keywords":["Magic"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"Your ferocity manifests into primordial power.","href":"ability/fury/level-8/primordial-rage/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Primordial Storm","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Each stormwight kit is associated with a primordial storm, which channels a specific damage type used by some of your abilities.","href":"fury/stormwight-kits/primordial-storm/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Primordial Storm: Anabatic Wind","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your primordial damage type is fire.","href":"fury/corven/primordial-storm-anabatic-wind/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Primordial Storm: Blizzard","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your primordial damage type is cold.","href":"fury/boren/primordial-storm-blizzard/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Primordial Storm: Lightning Storm","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your primordial damage type is lightning.","href":"fury/vuken/primordial-storm-lightning-storm/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Primordial Storm: Rat Flood","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your primordial damage type is corruption.","href":"fury/raden/primordial-storm-rat-flood/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Primordial Strength","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Berserker","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you damage an object with a weapon strike, the strike deals extra damage equal to your Might score. Additionally, whenever you push another creature into an object, the creature takes extra damage equal to your Might score.","href":"fury/level-1/primordial-strength/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Primordial Strike","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can manifest your ferocity directly as an elemental force created by the Primordial Chaos. As part of any strike, you can spend 1 ferocity to gain 1 surge that must be used for that strike. The extra damage dealt by the surge can be acid, cold, corruption, fire, lightning, poison, or sonic (your choice).","href":"fury/level-4/primordial-strike/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Primordial Vortex","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Berserker","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Ferocity","keywords":["Area","Magic","Melee","Weapon"],"distance":"**3** burst","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"You channel the power of the Primordial Chaos to pull foes to you.","href":"ability/fury/level-9/primordial-vortex/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Prism","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Essence","keywords":["Magic","Void"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"You split your essence, allowing you to cast multiple effects at once.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-9/prism/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Prismatic Scales","klass":"Dragon Knight","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Select one damage immunity granted by your Wyrmplate trait. You always have this immunity, in addition to the immunity granted by Wyrmplate.","href":"trait/dragon-knight/prismatic-scales/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Prophecy","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Oracle","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can better sift through the constant fragmentary visions from your deity and act to make them manifest. Each time you earn 1 or more Victories, you can make a number of 2d10 rolls equal to the number of Victories you earned. Record each roll in order. Then whenever you or a creature within 10 squares makes a power roll, you can use a free triggered action to replace the total on the dice with your first recorded roll.","href":"censor/level-5/prophecy/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Protective Circle","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Protection","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can spend 10 uninterrupted minutes to create a protective circle on the ground large enough to hold one size 1 creature. The circle lasts for 24 hours, until you create another, or until you dismiss it (no action required). Only creatures you designate at the time of drawing the circle can enter and exit the area. While in the protective circle, a creature can't be targeted by strikes.","href":"censor/level-1/protective-circle/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Protective Circle","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Protection","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can spend 10 uninterrupted minutes to create a protective circle on the ground large enough to hold one size 1 creature. The chosen character gains a bonus to their recovery value equal to your level that lasts until you finish another respite.","href":"conduit/level-1/revitalizing-ritual/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Ride","source":"other","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"A creature can take the Ride move action only while mounted on another creature (see Mounted Combat below). When a creature takes the Ride move action, they cause their mount to move up to the mount's speed, taking the rider with them. Alternatively, a creature can use the Ride move action to have their mount use the Disengage move action as a free triggered action. A creature can use the Ride move action only once per round. A mounted creature can only have this move action applied to them once per round. This movement can be broken up with the rider's maneuver and main action however they wish.","href":"common/move-actions/ride/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Ride or Die","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":8,"action":"main","cost":"11 Ferocity","keywords":["Beastheart"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"Your enemies might be stronger than you, but that's why you're not alone.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-8/ride-or-die/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Ride the Lightning","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Storm","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"Lightning and thunder infuse your body. Whenever you use an ability to deal rolled damage to another creature, the ability deals extra lightning damage equal to your Presence score. Additionally, if you use an ability that force moves a creature, the forced movement distance gains a bonus equal to your Presence score. While you are under the effect of your Windwalk feature, lightning enhances your locomotion to grant you a bonus to speed equal to your Might score. If Windwalk already grants you a bonus to speed, this bonus adds to that.","href":"censor/level-7/ride-the-lightning/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Riders on the Storm","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Stormwight","source":"class","level":6,"action":"maneuver","cost":"9 Ferocity","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"distance":"**3** aura","targets":"Each creature in the area","flavor":"You focus your connection to the Primordial Chaos into a seething storm.","href":"ability/fury/level-6/riders-on-the-storm/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Righteous Judgment","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":5,"action":"main","cost":"9 Wrath","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You amplify the power of your judgment.","href":"ability/censor/level-5/righteous-judgment/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Righteous Phalanx","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"War","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Piety","keywords":["Area","Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"**15** wall within **10**","targets":"Special","flavor":"A wall of spinning swords and knives appears where you wish.","href":"ability/conduit/level-9/righteous-phalanx/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Rip Them Apart!","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":5,"action":"main","cost":"9 Ferocity","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["bleeding","frightened","grabbed"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"In a gruesome display, you and your companion rip off a pinned enemy's limb or other body part and toss it away.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-5/rip-them-apart/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Riposte","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Duelist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"triggered","keywords":["Melee"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"I''d have brought treats had I known I''d be fighting a dog.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-1/riposte/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Ripples in the Earth","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"3 Essence","keywords":["Area","Earth","Magic"],"conditions":["prone"],"distance":"**2** burst","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"Like a stone was dropped into a pond, waves in the earth radiate from you.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-1/ripples-in-the-earth/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Rise!","klass":"Summoner","subclass":"Graves","feature_source":"circle","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Once per round, when a creature dies unwillingly within your Summoner's Range, you can use a triggered action to summon a signature undead minion in their space at no cost, even if you're at your minion maximum, but only if they can be organized into one of your squads. The new minion can't act until the start of your next turn.","href":"summoner/level-1/rise/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Rising Tension","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Auteur","source":"class","level":9,"action":"maneuver","cost":"11 Drama","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"conditions":["slowed","weakened"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One ally","flavor":"You narrate the tension of the scene and put all hope into your protagonist to turn things around.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-9/rising-tension/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Roar of the Crowd","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":9,"action":"trait","flavor":"You are empowered by your audience, near and far. You can't be made frightened, and if you are prone, you can stand up as a free maneuver.","href":"troubadour/level-9/roar-of-the-crowd/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Rock Smasher","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you deal rolled damage to a stoned creature while the basilisk is rampaging, you deal extra damage equal to twice your Might score.","href":"companion/beastheart/basilisk/level-6/rock-smasher/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Rolling Thunder","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Punisher","source":"class","level":6,"action":"main","cost":"9 Ferocity","keywords":["Companion","Magic","Melee","Strike"],"conditions":["prone"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"The rumble of your companion's dash is a rolling thunderclap, their impact an earthquake.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-6/rolling-thunder/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Rout","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Focus","conditions":["frightened"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"The tide begins to turn.","href":"ability/tactician/level-3/rout/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Routines","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You enter every battle with a set of performance abilities at the ready. Performances are magical presentations (such as songs, dances, poems, or gymnastic feats) that your allies can participate in. These abilities have the Performance keyword. At the start of each combat round, as long as you are not dazed, dead, or surprised, you can either choose a new performance or maintain your current performance (no action required). Your performance lasts until you are unable to maintain it or until the end of the encounter.","options":2,"href":"troubadour/level-1/routines/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Runaway Expansion","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"While the ball is rampaging, you and the ball have acid immunity 10, and whenever a creature is reduced to 0 Stamina while inside the ball, the ball's size increases by 1 (to a maximum of 5). The ball's size can't increase this way more than once a turn, and the ball shrinks back to their original size when their rampage ends.","href":"companion/beastheart/gummy-ball/level-10/runaway-expansion/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Sacred Bond","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Protection","source":"class","level":2,"action":"maneuver","cost":"5 Piety","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self and one ally","flavor":"You forge a divine connection between two creatures.","href":"ability/conduit/level-2/sacred-bond/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Sacrificial Offer","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Magic","Ranged","Strike"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Divine magic tears at your foe and defends a nearby friend.","href":"ability/conduit/level-1/sacrificial-offer/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Saint's Epiphany","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Knowledge","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"At the start of a respite, you can inspire yourself or another creature taking the same respite with divine knowledge. If the target makes a project roll during this respite, they can add 1d10 plus your Presence score to the roll.","href":"censor/level-4/saints-epiphany/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Saint's Raiment","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Piety","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One ally","flavor":"An ally becomes the wearer of an empowered golden cloak.","href":"ability/conduit/level-3/saints-raiment/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Saint's Tempest","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Storm","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Piety","keywords":["Area","Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"**3** cube within **10**","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"A raging storm appears, striking your foes with lightning and throwing them around with wind.","href":"ability/conduit/level-2/saints-tempest/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Saint's Vigilance","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Exorcist","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have honed your ability to detect sin and can use it to find those who hide from justice. Any creature judged by you can't use the Hide maneuver. Additionally, you gain an edge when searching for hidden creatures. If you find a hidden creature, you can use your Judgment ability against them as a free triggered action.","href":"censor/level-2/saints-vigilance/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Sanctified Weapon","klass":"Censor","subclass":"War","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"As a respite activity, you can bless a weapon. Any creature who wields the weapon gains a +1 bonus to rolled damage with abilities that use the weapon. This benefit lasts until you finish another respite.","href":"censor/level-1/sanctified-weapon/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Sanctified Weapon","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"War","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"As a respite activity, you can bless a weapon. Any creature who wields the weapon gains a +1 bonus to rolled damage with abilities that use the weapon. This benefit lasts until you finish another respite.","href":"conduit/level-1/sanctified-weapon/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Sanctuary","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":5,"action":"maneuver","cost":"9 Piety","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"You send yourself or an ally to a divine manifold to instantaneously regain health.","href":"ability/conduit/level-5/sanctuary/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Sanctuary Ward","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"In response to a foe's aggression, your god protects you. Whenever another creature damages you, that creature can't target you with a strike until you harm them or one of their allies, or until the end of their next turn.","href":"conduit/level-1/sanctuary-ward/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Saved in the Edit","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":8,"action":"maneuver","cost":"11 Drama","keywords":["Magic"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"You shout a word of power that allows you to rewrite reality to your whims.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-8/saved-in-the-edit/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Scalar Assault","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":8,"action":"main","cost":"11 Discipline","keywords":["Area","Psionic"],"distance":"**3** cube within **1**","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"You warp reality to grow a limb for just a moment and make a single devastating attack.","href":"ability/null/level-8/scalar-assault/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Scan","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can extend your psionic senses beyond their usual range. Once on each of your turns, you can search for hidden creatures as a free maneuver (see Hide and Sneak in Chapter 9: Tests). Additionally, once you establish line of effect to a thinking creature within distance of your Mind Spike ability, you always have line of effect to that creature until they move beyond that distance.","href":"talent/level-3/scan/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Scene Partner","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you obtain a success on a test to interact with an NPC using a skill from the interpersonal group, you can form a bond with that NPC. When you enter into a negotiation with a bonded NPC, their patience increases by 1 (to a maximum of 5). Additionally, the first time during a negotiation that you personally make an argument that would increase a bonded NPC's interest by 1, you instead increase their interest by 2 (to a maximum of 5).","href":"troubadour/level-1/scene-partner/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Seance","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Death","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can commune with a network of spirits. As a respite activity, you speak the name of a creature who died and isn't undead. If the creature's spirit is free and willing to speak with you, they appear and you can have a conversation with them. During this time, the creature responds to you as they would have in life. If the creature isn't free or willing to appear, you can speak another name or choose another respite activity.","href":"censor/level-4/seance/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Search for Hidden Creatures","source":"other","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"The Search for Hidden Creatures maneuver allows a creature to attempt to locate creatures hidden from them (see Hide and Sneak in Chapter 9: Tests).","href":"common/maneuvers/search-for-hidden-creatures/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Second Album","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Virtuoso","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following performance abilities, which are usable with your Routines feature.","options":2,"href":"troubadour/level-3/second-album/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"See Through Their Tricks","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Reaver","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have a double edge on tests made to search for hidden creatures, discern hidden motives, or detect lies. You also have a double edge on tests made to gamble!","href":"fury/level-3/see-through-their-tricks/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"See Your Enemies Driven Before You","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Vanguard","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have trained your squad to maximize impact and break enemy lines when they attack.","href":"tactician/level-8/see-your-enemies-driven-before-you/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Seek and Destroy","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Reaver","source":"class","level":6,"action":"main","cost":"9 Ferocity","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["frightened"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You break through the enemy lines to make an example.","href":"ability/fury/level-6/seek-and-destroy/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Seize the Initiative","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"If you are not surprised when combat begins, your side gets to go first. If an enemy has an ability that allows their side to go first, you roll as usual to determine who goes first.","href":"tactician/level-7/seize-the-initiative/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Sentenced","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Paragon","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Wrath","keywords":["Magic","Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["restrained"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"The shock of your condemnation freezes your enemy in their boots.","href":"ability/censor/level-2/sentenced/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Sermon of Grace","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"5 Piety","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"conditions":["prone"],"distance":"**4** burst","targets":"Each ally in the area","flavor":"You inspire your allies with tales of your saint's great deeds.","href":"ability/conduit/level-1/sermon-of-grace/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Setup","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"5 Insight","keywords":["Ranged","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Ranged **5**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Your friends will thank you.","href":"ability/shadow/level-1/setup/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Shadow Abilities","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Choose one signature ability from the following options. Signature abilities can be used at will. (Quick Build: Teamwork Has Its Place.)","options":3,"href":"shadow/level-1/shadow-abilities/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Shadow College","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Shadow colleges are secret institutions that turn ordinary folk into something else. Finding a college is the first step in a rigorous initiation process that tests the mettle of an applicant. Even those who make the cut often wash out—or are kicked out—as the master shadows who teach stealth, magic, and assassination to their students are often less than gentle in their approach.","href":"shadow/level-1/shadow-college/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Shadow in the Mist","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Prowler","source":"class","level":1,"action":"triggered","keywords":["Magic"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"While everyone's eyes are drawn to your foe, you wreathe yourself in obscuring mist.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-1/shadow-in-the-mist/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Shadowfall","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":5,"action":"main","cost":"9 Insight","keywords":["Area","Melee","Weapon"],"distance":"**10** x **1** line within **1**","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"You vanish. They fall. You reappear.","href":"ability/shadow/level-5/shadowfall/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Shadowgrasp","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":8,"action":"main","cost":"11 Insight","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"conditions":["restrained"],"distance":"**2** burst","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"The shadows around you give way, allowing the shadow creature within you to grasp at your foes.","href":"ability/shadow/level-8/shadowgrasp/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Shadowmeld","klass":"Polder","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"maneuver","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Magic"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"You become an actual shadow.","href":"ability/polder/shadowmeld/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Shadowstrike","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"5 Insight","keywords":["Magic","Melee","Ranged"],"distance":"Self; see below","targets":"Self","flavor":"They have no idea what the college taught you.","href":"ability/shadow/level-1/shadowstrike/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Shake It Off","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Vanguard","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"As a free maneuver, you can spend 1d6 Stamina to ignore a consequence from a test, or to end one effect on you that is ended by a saving throw or that ends at the end of your turn. Any ally adjacent to you can also spend Stamina as a free maneuver to gain this benefit.","href":"tactician/level-5/shake-it-off/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Shaping","klass":"Summoner","subclass":"Blight","feature_source":"circle","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can spend 1 uninterrupted minute to perform a ritual that causes one of your minions to fold their shape and disguise themself to look like a duplicate of you, including speaking basic Caelian, allowing them to (potentially) freely move through civilization while completing their tasks. You can have a number of minions disguised at the same time equal to your Reason score.","href":"summoner/level-5/shaping/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Shared Momentum","klass":"Null","subclass":"Chronokinetic","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"When you take the Disengage move action, one ally in the area of your Null Field ability can also take the Disengage move action as a free triggered action, using your distance for that move action.","href":"null/level-8/shared-momentum/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Shared Power","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"If the ability targets individual creatures or objects, you target one additional creature or object within distance.","href":"null/level-7/shared-power/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Shared Power","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"If the ability targets individual creatures or objects, you target one additional creature or object within distance.","href":"talent/level-6/shared-power/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Shared Scales","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have immunity 3 to the drake's attuned damage type.","href":"companion/beastheart/drake/level-1/shared-scales/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Shared Void Sense","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Void","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","keywords":["Magic","Ranged","Void"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Special","flavor":"You grant allies a taste of your unearthly vision.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-1/shared-void-sense/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Sharpened Power","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"If the ability has any power roll, that roll gains an edge.","href":"null/level-7/sharpened-power/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Sharpened Power","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"If the ability has any power roll, that roll gains an edge.","href":"talent/level-6/sharpened-power/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Shield of the Righteous","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":5,"action":"main","cost":"9 Wrath","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You strike a foe and create a fleet of divine shields that protect your allies.","href":"ability/censor/level-5/shield-of-the-righteous/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Shield!","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"triggered","distance":"Summoner's Range","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"You call upon a minion to use their body to dampen the blow.","href":"ability/summoner/level-1/shield/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Shield!","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following triggered action.","href":"summoner/level-1/shield/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Shieldbreaker","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":3,"action":"main","cost":"7 Ferocity","keywords":["Beastheart","Melee","Ranged","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1** or ranged **5**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You smash through their guard and shatter their armor, leaving them wide open.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-3/shieldbreaker/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Shields of Essence","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Summoner's Range","targets":"Special","flavor":"You call forth protective forces to keep you all from harm.","href":"ability/summoner/level-1/shields-of-essence/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Shining Armor (Protective Attack)","klass":"Shining Armor","source":"kit","level":0,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["taunted"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"The strength of your assault makes it impossible for your foe to ignore you.","href":"ability/Kits/shining-armor-protective-attack/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Shock and Awe","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Vanguard","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have expanded your leadership skills, strengthening your followers' morale and providing logistical support. During a montage test or negotiation, you can obtain one automatic success on a test made using a skill from the interpersonal skill group. Additionally, you can convince a group of people to help you with a crafting project during a respite. If these people are available when you take a respite, you can make a project roll for a crafting project in addition to undertaking another respite activity.","href":"tactician/level-7/shock-and-awe/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Shower of Blood","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Reaver","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Ferocity","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You shock your foes with the brutality of your strike, resetting the balance of combat.","href":"ability/fury/level-9/shower-of-blood/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Sic 'Em!","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Guardian","source":"class","level":6,"action":"main","cost":"9 Ferocity","keywords":["Charge","Companion","Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["prone","taunted"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Your companion rushes forward to protect you from a dangerous foe.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-6/sic-em/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Signature Ability","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Choose one signature ability from the following options. Signature abilities can be used at will. (Quick Build: Witty Banter.)","options":5,"href":"troubadour/level-1/signature-ability/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Signature Trait: Big!","klass":"Hakaan","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your stature reflects your giant forebears. Your size is 1L.","href":"trait/hakaan/big/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Signature Trait: Detect the Supernatural","klass":"Human","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"As a maneuver, you can open your awareness to detect supernatural creatures and phenomena. Until the end of your next turn, you know the location of any supernatural object, or any undead, construct, or creature from another world within 5 squares, even if you don't have line of effect to that object or creature. You know if you're detecting an item or a creature, and you know the nature of any creature you detect.","href":"trait/human/detect-the-supernatural/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Signature Trait: Fall Lightly","klass":"Memonek","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your silicone body is low in density. Whenever you fall, you reduce the distance of the fall by 2 squares.","href":"trait/memonek/fall-lightly/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Signature Trait: Former Life","klass":"Revenant","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Choose the ancestry you were before you died. Your size is that ancestry's size and your speed is 5. Unless you select one of the Previous Life traits (see below), you don't receive any other ancestral traits from your original ancestry.","href":"trait/revenant/former-life/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Signature Trait: High Elf Glamor","klass":"High Elf","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"A magic glamor makes others perceive you as interesting and engaging, granting you an edge on Presence tests using the Flirt or Persuade skills. This glamor makes you appear and sound slightly different to each creature you meet, since what is engaging to one might be different for another. However, you never appear to be anyone other than yourself.","href":"trait/high-elf/high-elf-glamor/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Signature Trait: Lightweight","klass":"Memonek","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your body is light for a creature of your height. Whenever another creature attempts to force move you, you treat your size as one size smaller than it is.","href":"trait/memonek/lightweight/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Signature Trait: Psychic Scar","klass":"Time Raider","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your mind is a formidable layer of defense. You have psychic immunity equal to your level.","href":"trait/time-raider/psychic-scar/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Signature Trait: Relentless","klass":"Orc","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever a creature deals damage to you that leaves you dying, you can make a free strike against any creature. If the creature is reduced to 0 Stamina by your strike, you can spend a Recovery.","href":"trait/orc/relentless/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Signature Trait: Runic Carving","klass":"Dwarf","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can carve a rune onto your skin with 10 uninterrupted minutes of work, which is activated by the magic within your body. The rune you carve determines the benefit you receive, chosen from among the following:","href":"trait/dwarf/runic-carving/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Signature Trait: Shadowmeld","klass":"Polder","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following ability.","grants":"the Shadowmeld maneuver","href":"trait/polder/shadowmeld/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Signature Trait: Silver Tongue","klass":"Devil","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your innate magic allows you to twist how your words are perceived to get a better read on people and convince them to see things your way. You have one skill of your choice from the interpersonal skill group (see Skills in Chapter 9: Tests), and you gain an edge on tests when attempting to discover an NPC's motivations and pitfalls during a negotiation (see Chapter 11: Negotiation).","href":"trait/devil/silver-tongue/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Signature Trait: Small!","klass":"Polder","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your diminutive stature lets you easily get out of—or into—trouble. Your size is 1S.","href":"trait/polder/small/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Signature Trait: Tough But Withered","klass":"Revenant","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your undead body grants you immunity to cold, corruption, lightning, and poison damage equal to your level, but you have fire weakness 5. You can't suffocate, and you don't need to eat or drink to stay alive.","href":"trait/revenant/tough-but-withered/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Signature Trait: Wode Elf Glamor","klass":"Wode Elf","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can magically alter your appearance to better blend in with your surroundings. You can use the Hide maneuver even if you are observed and don't initially have cover or concealment. When you do so, you can shift a number of squares equal to your Agility score. If you end this movement with cover or concealment, you are automatically hidden.","href":"shadow/level-1/smoke-bomb/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Smolder","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"3 flake.","href":"ability/talent/level-1/smolder/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Smoldering Step","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Fire","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can use area.","href":"elementalist/level-5/smoldering-step/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Snare pull that creature toward one of your minions within your Summoner's Range a number of squares equal to your Reason score.","href":"summoner/level-3/snare-ward/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Sniper husk.","href":"ability/talent/level-3/soul-burn/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Soul Flense","klass":"Summoner","subclass":"Blight","feature_source":"circle","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"As a maneuver, you can command one or more of your demon minions to each deal damage equal to their free strike value to an adjacent ally. This damage can't be reduced. The ally then ends a condition affecting them and confers it to the demon that attacked them.","href":"summoner/level-5/soul-flense/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Soul Rancor","source":"other","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"You gain a surge the first time in a round that your demon minions deal 3 or more damage to a creature while you have line of effect to the boil. You can choose to give the surge to an ally who also has line of effect to the boil.","href":"fixture/demon/the-boil/level-5/soul-rancor/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Soul Siphon","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":3,"action":"main","cost":"7 Piety","keywords":["Magic","Ranged","Strike"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One enemy","flavor":"A beam of energy connects a foe to a friend, draining life from one to heal the other.","href":"ability/conduit/level-3/soul-siphon/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Soulbound","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":5,"action":"main","cost":"9 Clarity","keywords":["Animapathy","Psionic","Ranged","Strike"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Two enemies","flavor":"You fire a piercing bolt of psychic energy that lances through two foes and leaves a faint intangible thread between them.","href":"ability/talent/level-5/soulbound/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Soulsense","klass":"Summoner","subclass":"Blight","feature_source":"circle","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"While you have line of effect to a creature with a soul, you can perceive a trail of where the creature has been in the last number of minutes equal to 5 × your level.","href":"summoner/level-1/soulsense/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Spark Off Your Skin","klass":"Dwarf","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your stone skin affords you potent protection. Lightning)","klass":"Spellsword","source":"kit","level":0,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Magic","Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature or within **5**","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"Animal spirits run through the battlefield, trampling your foes.","href":"ability/conduit/level-6/spirit-stampede/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Spirit object","flavor":"You form a blade of mind energy and stab your target, invigorating yourself.","href":"ability/talent/level-1/spirit-sword/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Spirit Ward","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Invisible spirits surround you if you are harmed. Whenever an adjacent creature deals damage to you, they take corruption damage equal to your Intuition score.","href":"conduit/level-1/spirit-ward/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Spiteful Endurance","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"While the boar is winded, they have damage immunity equal to their Might score and ignore the effects of bleeding.","href":"companion/beastheart/boar/level-1/spiteful-endurance/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Split","klass":"Summoner","subclass":"Storms","feature_source":"circle","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"Once during your turn, you can use a free maneuver to deal damage to one of your elemental minions equal to half their maximum Stamina in order to create one additional copy of that minion in an adjacent unoccupied space and add them to their squad, even if you're at your minion maximum. You can't use this feature if it would kill one or more of the minions in the squad.","href":"summoner/level-5/split/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Spore Puff","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Companion","Melee"],"conditions":["dazed"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One enemy","flavor":"The sporeling breathes a cloud of disorienting fumes.","href":"ability/companion/beastheart/sporeling/level-1/spore-puff/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Spotlight","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":6,"action":"none","keywords":["Area","Magic","Performance"],"distance":"**5** aura","targets":"Self and each ally in the area","flavor":"The audience is watching, so you'd better give them a show.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-6/spotlight/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Spotlight","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following performance ability, which is usable with your Routines feature.","grants":"the Spotlight no action","href":"troubadour/level-6/spotlight/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Squad! Forward!","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","cost":"3 Focus","keywords":["Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self and two allies","flavor":"On your command, you and your allies force back the enemy line.","href":"ability/tactician/level-1/squad-forward/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Squad! Gear Check!","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":5,"action":"main","cost":"9 Focus","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You distract a foe while your allies secure their defensive gear.","href":"ability/tactician/level-5/squad-gear-check/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Squad!
