On Me!","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Vanguard","source":"class","level":2,"action":"maneuver","cost":"5 Focus","keywords":["Area"],"distance":"**1** burst","targets":"Self and each ally in the area","flavor":"Together we are invincible!","href":"ability/tactician/level-2/squad-on-me/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Squad! Remember Your Training!","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":5,"action":"main","cost":"9 Focus","keywords":["Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self and two allies","flavor":"You remind your allies how to best use their gear.","href":"ability/tactician/level-5/squad-remember-your-training/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Stabilizing Field","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Discipline","keywords":["Psionic"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"You project order, making it harder for your enemies to interfere with you and your allies.","href":"ability/null/level-3/stabilizing-field/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Staggering Blow","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":3,"action":"main","cost":"7 Insight","keywords":["Melee","Ranged","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["prone","slowed"],"distance":"Melee **1** or ranged **5**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"There's no recovering from this.","href":"ability/shadow/level-3/staggering-blow/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Staggering Curse","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Magic","Melee","Strike"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"A blast of judgment disorients your foe.","href":"ability/conduit/level-1/staggering-curse/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Stalwart Icon","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Paragon","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"You exhibit a small spark of your deity's power, causing creatures to trust or fear you, depending on what you need. You gain an edge on tests made to intimidate or persuade others.","href":"censor/level-2/stalwart-icon/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Stand Fast!","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Paragon","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your divine spark grows in power, allowing you and your allies to focus and endure. At the start of each of your turns, you can spend 1d6 Stamina to end one effect on you that is ended by a saving throw or that ends at the end of your turn. Any ally who starts their turn within 5 squares of you can also spend Stamina to gain this benefit.","href":"censor/level-5/stand-fast/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Stand Tough","klass":"Dwarf","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your body is made to withstand the blows of your enemies. Your Might score is treated as 1 higher for the purpose of resisting potencies, and you gain an edge on Might tests when called for to resist environmental effects or a creature's traits or abilities.","href":"trait/dwarf/stand-tough/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Stand Tough","klass":"Hakaan","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your body is made to withstand the blows of your enemies. Your Might score is treated as 1 higher for the purpose of resisting potencies, and you gain an edge on Might tests when called for to resist environmental effects or a creature's traits or abilities.","href":"trait/hakaan/stand-tough/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Stand Up","source":"other","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"A creature can use the Stand Up maneuver to stand up if they are prone, ending that condition. Alternatively, they can use this maneuver to make a willing adjacent prone creature stand up.","href":"common/maneuvers/stand-up/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Star Power","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Duelist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","cost":"1 Drama","distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"Your years of practicing fencing and dancing pay off on the battlefield.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-1/star-power/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Star Power","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Duelist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following ability.","grants":"the Star Power maneuver","href":"troubadour/level-1/star-power/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Star Solo","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":3,"action":"main","cost":"7 Drama","keywords":["Magic","Melee","Ranged","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1** or ranged **10**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"Your performance travels and doesn't stop moving until your audience is completely rocked.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-3/star-solo/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Stare Down","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Guardian","source":"class","level":6,"action":"maneuver","cost":"9 Ferocity","keywords":["Companion","Magic","Ranged"],"conditions":["frightened","weakened"],"distance":"Ranged **5**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Your companion locks eyes with an enemy, imposing their will upon the enemy and daring them to move a muscle.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-6/stare-down/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Stasis Field","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Chronopathy","source":"class","level":6,"action":"main","cost":"9 Clarity","keywords":["Area","Chronopathy","Psionic","Ranged"],"conditions":["restrained","slowed"],"distance":"**4** cube within **10**","targets":"Each creature and object in the area","flavor":"Keep everything as it was. Ignore everything that will be.","href":"ability/talent/level-6/stasis-field/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Stasis Shield","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Chronopathy","source":"class","level":8,"action":"triggered","cost":"3 Clarity","keywords":["Psionic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self, or one creature or object","flavor":"You freeze time just long enough to bring the victim to safety!","href":"ability/talent/level-8/stasis-shield/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Stasis Shield","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Chronopathy","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following ability.","grants":"the Stasis Shield triggered action","href":"talent/level-8/stasis-shield/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Static Shock","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Companion","Magic","Melee"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"An arc of lightning crackles from the spark.","href":"ability/companion/beastheart/elemental-spark/level-1/static-shock/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Statue of Power","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Creation","source":"class","level":2,"action":"maneuver","cost":"5 Piety","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Special","flavor":"A marble statue of your deity rises from the earth.","href":"ability/conduit/level-2/statue-of-power/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Stay Strong and Focus!","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Focus","distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"We can do this! Keep faith and hold fast!","href":"ability/tactician/level-3/stay-strong-and-focus/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Staying Power","klass":"Human","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your human physiology allows you to fight, run, and stay awake longer than others. You increase your number of Recoveries by 2.","href":"trait/human/staying-power/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Steel","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":8,"action":"maneuver","cost":"11 Clarity","keywords":["Metamorphosis","Psionic","Ranged"],"conditions":["slowed","weakened"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"The target's skin becomes covered in tough metal.","href":"ability/talent/level-8/steel/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Steel Ward","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your ward reacts to danger, protecting you from future harm. Whenever you take damage, after the damage resolves, you gain damage immunity equal to your Reason score until the end of your next turn.","href":"talent/level-1/steel-ward/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Steelbreaker","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Ferocity","keywords":["Magic"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"See how useless their weapons are!","href":"ability/fury/level-3/steelbreaker/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Steward of Two Worlds","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":9,"action":"trait","flavor":"You and your allies are now welcome in your circle's source manifold. Negotiations with native denizens of your circle's source manifold have their patience increased by 2.","href":"summoner/level-9/steward-of-two-worlds/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Stick And Robe (Where I Want You)","klass":"Stick And Robe","source":"kit","level":0,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **2**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"When your stick speaks, your enemy moves.","href":"ability/Kits/stick-and-robe-where-i-want-you/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Sticky Bomb","klass":"Shadow","subclass":"Caustic Alchemy","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Insight","keywords":["Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Explosives are best when they're attached to an enemy.","href":"ability/shadow/level-2/sticky-bomb/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Stink Bomb","klass":"Shadow","subclass":"Caustic Alchemy","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Insight","keywords":["Area","Ranged"],"conditions":["weakened"],"distance":"**3** cube within **10**","targets":"Each creature in the area","flavor":"Putrid yellow gas explodes from a bomb you toss.","href":"ability/shadow/level-2/stink-bomb/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Stone Singer","klass":"Dwarf","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have a magic connection to the earth. When you spend 1 uninterrupted hour singing, you can reshape any unworked mundane stone within 3 squares. You can't destroy this stone, but you can move each square of it anywhere within 3 squares, piling it off to one side to dig a hole or building it up to create a wall.","href":"trait/dwarf/stone-singer/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Stoned","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"A stoned creature is magically turning to stone. Each time a creature fails the saving throw to end this effect, they take corruption damage equal to the basilisk's Might score. A stoned creature or a creature adjacent to them can use a maneuver to cut the encroaching stone from the stoned target's body, ending the effect and dealing damage to the target equal to twice the basilisk's Might score that can't be reduced in any way. A creature reduced to 0 Stamina while they are stoned, or by an ability that causes a creature to become stoned, is turned to stone until they are restored to life by magical means.","href":"companion/beastheart/basilisk/level-1/stoned/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Storm of Sands","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":5,"action":"main","cost":"9 Essence","keywords":["Area","Earth","Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"**4** cube within **10**","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"Dirt and debris swirl into a dark, pulsing hurricane.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-5/storm-of-sands/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Stormborn","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Stormwight","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"You and each ally within 5 squares of you ignore negative effects from inclement weather, such as banes or environmental damage. Additionally, you can use the Blessing of Fortunate Weather feature as if you were a 1st-level conduit (see 1st-Level Domain Feature in the Conduit section).","href":"fury/level-5/stormborn/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Stormheart","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Spark","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you or your companion deals cold, fire, lightning, sonic, or untyped damage, you can change the damage type to cold, fire, lightning, or sonic damage.","href":"beastheart/level-2/stormheart/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Stormrage","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Beastheart","Magic","Melee","Ranged","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1** or ranged **5**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"Lances of primordial energy leap from you and your companion to sear, crush, or freeze your foe.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-1/stormrage/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Strike for Me","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"triggered","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Summoner's Range","targets":"Each of your minions","flavor":"Your minions fight in your stead.","href":"ability/summoner/level-1/strike-for-me/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Strike For Me","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following free triggered action.","grants":"the Strike for Me free triggered action","href":"summoner/level-1/strike-for-me/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Strike Now","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following ability.","grants":"the \"Strike Now!\" main action","href":"tactician/level-1/strike-now/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Strike Now!","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","keywords":["Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One ally","flavor":"Your foe left an opening. You point this out to an ally!","href":"ability/tactician/level-1/strike-now/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Strong Like Bear","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You gain a +1 bonus to your stability.","href":"companion/beastheart/bear/level-1/strong-like-bear/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Strongest There Is","klass":"Fury","subclass":"Berserker","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your strength is unmatched. Whenever you make a Might test, you can roll three dice and choose which two to use. Additionally, whenever you use the Knockback maneuver, the forced movement distance gains a bonus equal to your Might score.","href":"fury/level-8/strongest-there-is/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Studied Commander","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Mastermind","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Additionally, if you have 24 hours or more before a combat encounter or negotiation, and you have one or more clues or rumors regarding the encounter or negotiation, you can make a Reason test as a respite activity. The following test outcomes apply to a combat encounter:","href":"tactician/level-1/studied-commander/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Stunning Blow","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"3 Discipline","keywords":["Melee","Psionic","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["dazed","slowed"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"You focus your psionic technique into a concussive punch.","href":"ability/null/level-1/stunning-blow/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Subterfuge","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have an epic resource called subterfuge. Each time you finish a respite, you gain subterfuge equal to the XP you gain. You can spend subterfuge on your abilities as if it were insight.","href":"shadow/level-10/subterfuge/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Subtle Relocation","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Void","source":"class","level":1,"action":"triggered","keywords":["Magic","Ranged","Void"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"You call on the void to swallow and spit out an ally.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-1/subtle-relocation/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Subvert the Green Within","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Essence","keywords":["Green","Magic","Ranged","Strike","Void"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Fungal spores sprout inside your enemy's brain, allowing you to control their actions.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-2/subvert-the-green-within/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Subverted Perception of Space","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":5,"action":"main","cost":"9 Essence","keywords":["Magic","Ranged","Strike","Void"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"You rip an enemy's world in twain.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-5/subverted-perception-of-space/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Suck It Up","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever the ball makes a strike while rampaging, one target is pulled up to 3 squares into the ball's space. If the target ends this movement and completely fits within the ball's space, the target is grabbed by the ball.","href":"companion/beastheart/gummy-ball/level-3/suck-it-up/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Summon Source of Earth","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Earth","source":"class","level":8,"action":"main","keywords":["Earth","Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Special","flavor":"The ground rumbles as an elemental bursts forth, ready to serve.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-8/summon-source-of-earth/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Summon Source of Earth","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Earth","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","href":"elementalist/level-8/summon-source-of-earth/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Summoner Abilities","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Choose one heroic ability from the following options, each of which costs 5 essence to use. (Quick Build: Rallying Cry.)","options":1,"href":"summoner/level-1/summoner-abilities/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Summoner Circle","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"circle","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You've learned the art of summoning from a member of a loose network of summoners called a circle. Each circle specializes in a distinct portfolio of creatures you can call into existence. Choose a summoner circle from the following options, each of which grants you a communication feature (Quick Build: Circle of Graves.)","href":"summoner/level-1/summoner-circle/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Summoner Strike","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","keywords":["Magic","Melee","Ranged","Strike"],"conditions":["slowed"],"distance":"Melee **1** or Ranged **5**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"A sudden burst of energy erupts from your implement and shocks your foes' nerves.","href":"ability/summoner/level-1/summoner-strike/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Summoner Strike","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following ability, which replaces both your melee and ranged free strikes.","grants":"the Summoner Strike main action","href":"summoner/level-1/summoner-strike/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Summoner's Dominion","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"circle","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","href":"summoner/level-2/summoners-dominion/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Summoner's Kit","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"You conjure a kit for yourself. This kit includes an implement, such as a rod or a baton, which grants you the following benefits:","options":4,"href":"summoner/level-3/summoners-kit/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Summoner's Sword","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","keywords":["Magic","Melee","Strike"],"distance":"Melee **3**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"You draw your strength from the army you surround yourself with and summon a hot blade of energy and fervor.","href":"ability/summoner/level-1/summoners-sword/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Supersniffer","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Prowler","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"While a creature is adjacent to your companion, that creature can't be hidden or have concealment from your companion.","href":"beastheart/level-2/supersniffer/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Surge of Insight","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"The first time each combat round that you deal damage incorporating 1 or more surges, you gain 2 insight instead of 1.","href":"shadow/level-4/surge-of-insight/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Surging Essence","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"When you gain essence at the start of each of your turns during combat, you gain 3 essence instead of 2.","href":"elementalist/level-7/surging-essence/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Suspensor Field","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can fly. While flying, your stability is reduced to 0 and can't be increased. If you can already fly, you have a +2 bonus to speed while flying instead.","href":"talent/level-4/suspensor-field/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Swarm of Spirits","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":3,"action":"main","cost":"7 Essence","keywords":["Area","Green","Magic"],"distance":"**3** aura","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"Guardian animal spirits surround you to harry your foes and bolster your allies.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-3/swarm-of-spirits/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Swashbuckler (Fancy Footwork)","klass":"Swashbuckler","source":"kit","level":0,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"All combat is a dance—and you'll be the one leading.","href":"ability/Kits/swashbuckler-fancy-footwork/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Swift","klass":"Wode Elf","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have speed 6.","href":"trait/wode-elf/swift/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Sword And Board (Shield Bash)","klass":"Sword And Board","source":"kit","level":0,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["prone"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"In your hands, a shield isn't just for protection.","href":"ability/Kits/sword-and-board-shield-bash/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Synapse Field","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Discipline","keywords":["Psionic"],"distance":"Self","targets":"Self","flavor":"Attacks made by allies in your null field disrupt your enemies' thoughts, causing psychic pain.","href":"ability/null/level-3/synapse-field/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Synaptic Anchor","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":8,"action":"triggered","cost":"11 Discipline","keywords":["Psionic"],"conditions":["dazed"],"distance":"Self; see below","targets":"Self or one creature","flavor":"You disrupt an enemy's strike and create a feedback loop in their mind, preventing them from focusing on future attacks.","href":"ability/null/level-8/synaptic-anchor/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Synaptic Conditioning","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Telepathy","source":"class","level":6,"action":"main","cost":"9 Clarity","keywords":["Psionic","Melee","Strike","Telepathy"],"distance":"Melee **2**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"It's a subtle mindset shift. It's not that they're your enemy—you just don't like them!","href":"ability/talent/level-6/synaptic-conditioning/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Synaptic Dissipation","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Telepathy","source":"class","level":6,"action":"maneuver","cost":"9 Clarity","keywords":["Psionic","Ranged","Strike","Telepathy"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Special","flavor":"You manipulate your enemies' minds and make them wonder if you were ever really there in the first place.","href":"ability/talent/level-6/synaptic-dissipation/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Synaptic Override","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Telepathy","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Clarity","keywords":["Psionic","Ranged","Telepathy"],"conditions":["weakened"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One enemy","flavor":"You control an enemy's nervous system. How pleasant for them.","href":"ability/talent/level-2/synaptic-override/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Synaptic Reset","klass":"Null","source":"class","level":5,"action":"maneuver","cost":"9 Discipline","keywords":["Area","Psionic"],"distance":"**3** burst","targets":"Self and each ally in the area","flavor":"You expand your nullifying power to mitigate harmful effects.","href":"ability/null/level-5/synaptic-reset/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Synaptic Terror","klass":"Talent","subclass":"Telepathy","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Clarity","keywords":["Area","Psionic","Telepathy"],"conditions":["frightened"],"distance":"**3** burst","targets":"Each ally and enemy in the area","flavor":"You project a terrifying image into the brains of your foes, and their fear psionically invigorates your allies.","href":"ability/talent/level-9/synaptic-terror/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Synaptic Triage","klass":"Null","subclass":"Cryokinetic","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"As a free maneuver, you can spend 1d6 Stamina to remove one effect on you. Each creature of your choice in the area of your Null Field ability also gains this benefit.","href":"null/level-8/synaptic-triage/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Systematic Mind","klass":"Memonek","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"You gain an edge on tests made to parse schematics, maps, and other systematic documents that aren't inherently chaotic. In addition, you treat any language you don't know as if you know a related language.","href":"trait/memonek/systematic-mind/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Tactical Doctrine","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Warfare is as old as civilization—and perhaps even older. As battle became ever more complex, military leaders invented tactical doctrine, outlining how combatants should be structured, used, and deployed. Doctrine can be learned at war colleges passing on ancient martial traditions, or directly through blood and sweat on the battlefield. Whatever path brought you to your mastery of historically proven tactics, you choose a tactical doctrine from the following options, each of which grants you a skill. (Quick Build: Vanguard and the Intimidate skill.)","href":"tactician/level-1/tactical-doctrine/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Tactical Offensive","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Vanguard","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"When you use the Charge main action to attack a creature marked by you, you can use a signature or heroic ability with the Melee and Strike keywords instead of a melee free strike.","href":"tactician/level-5/tactical-offensive/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Tactician Abilities","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Choose one heroic ability from the following options, each of which costs 3 focus to use. (Quick Build: Inspiring Strike.)","options":2,"href":"tactician/level-1/tactician-abilities/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Taffy Pull","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"While the ball is rampaging, your arms and legs become viscous and stretchy, and you gain a +2 bonus to speed and melee distance.","href":"companion/beastheart/gummy-ball/level-6/taffy-pull/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Take Two!","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Auteur","source":"class","level":5,"action":"none","keywords":["Area","Magic","Performance"],"distance":"**5** aura","targets":"Self and each ally in the area","flavor":"One more, and this time make it interesting.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-5/take-two/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Talent Abilities","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Choose two signature abilities from the following options. Signature abilities can be used at will. (Quick Build: Incinerate, Kinetic Grip.)","options":2,"href":"talent/level-1/talent-abilities/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Talent Tradition","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Psionic abilities are grouped into categories according to their effects, with each category comprising one of many talent traditions:","href":"talent/level-1/talent-tradition/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Talent Ward","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Through meditation, you create a ward that protects you. Choose one of the following wards. You can change your ward and psionic augmentation (see above) by undergoing a psionic meditation as a respite activity. (Quick Build: Repulsive Ward.)","options":4,"href":"talent/level-1/talent-ward/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Targets of Opportunity","klass":"Tactician","subclass":"Mastermind","source":"class","level":2,"action":"maneuver","cost":"5 Focus","keywords":["Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **5**","targets":"Two creatures","flavor":"You point out easy targets to your friends, allowing them to include more enemies in their attacks.","href":"ability/tactician/level-2/targets-of-opportunity/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Teamwork Has Its Place","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Melee","Ranged","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1** or ranged **5**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"You attack an enemy as an ally exposes their weakness.","href":"ability/shadow/level-1/teamwork-has-its-place/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Tear You to Ribbons","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":3,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever the deinonychus makes a strike against a creature while rampaging, the target is bleeding until the end of their next turn.","href":"companion/beastheart/deinonychus/level-3/tear-you-to-ribbons/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Telepathic Speech","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You know the Mindspeech language (see Languages in Orden in Chapter 4: Background). Additionally, you can telepathically communicate with any creatures within distance of your Mind Spike ability if they share a language with you and you know of each other. When you communicate with someone this way, they can respond telepathically.","href":"talent/level-1/telepathic-speech/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Templar","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"You are the ultimate representation of your god's justice in the timescape. Whenever you use your Judgment ability, you can use a free triggered action to use a conduit domain effect (see Domain Piety and Effects in the Conduit section) associated with your chosen domain, or a domain you access with virtue (see below). If the effect calls for the use of your Intuition score, you use your Presence score instead. If the effect uses your conduit level, use your censor level instead.","href":"censor/level-10/templar/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Terra Resonance","source":"other","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"Each round, you gain a surge the first time an area of terrain gains a supernatural effect (excluding auras) while you have line of effect to the crystal. You can choose to give the surge to an ally who also has line of effect to the crystal.","href":"fixture/elemental/primordial-crystal/level-5/terra-resonance/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Terrible Claws","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Companion","Melee","Weapon"],"conditions":["bleeding"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One enemy","flavor":"The deinonychus kicks their prey, then slashes them with wicked claws.","href":"ability/companion/beastheart/deinonychus/level-1/terrible-claws/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Terror The target comes back with 0 Stamina plus 1 Stamina for each minion and essence used in the effect. You must have at least one fragment of the creature's remains, and the creature's soul must be willing to return to life for the effect to work.","href":"summoner/level-7/their-life-for-mine/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Their Pall Shrouds All","klass":"Summoner","feature_source":"summoner","source":"class","level":6,"action":"maneuver","keywords":["Area","Champion","Magic"],"conditions":["weakened"],"distance":"**4** burst","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"Your champion fills the area with a thick haze hiding friend from foe.","href":"ability/summoner/level-6/their-pall-shrouds-all/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Their Past Revealed","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Oracle","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your constant fragmentary visions become clearer, and can be honed to understand the past of creatures you interact with. While speaking with any creature, you can make a medium Presence test to see visions from their past. On a success, you see a clear view of any subject related to the creature's past that you wish to understand. On a success with a consequence, you see two visions, one false and one true. On a failure, you lose 2d6 Stamina.","href":"censor/level-8/their-past-revealed/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"There For Each (Quick Build: Virtuoso.)","href":"troubadour/level-1/troubadour-class-act/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"True Focus","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"When you Additionally, you can jump as part of your charge.","href":"fury/level-2/unstoppable-force/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Unstoppable Mind","klass":"High Elf","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your mind allows you to maintain your focus in any situation. You can't be made dazed.","href":"trait/high-elf/unstoppable-mind/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Upstage","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","cost":"3 Drama","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["prone","taunted"],"distance":"Self; see below","targets":"Self","flavor":"As you bob and weave through the crowd, you can't help but leave the audience wanting more.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-1/upstage/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Ursine Form","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"While the bear is rampaging, you have damage immunity 5 and your size increases to the bear's size (to a maximum of 2). If you don't have enough unoccupied space to grow, you grow as soon as there is sufficient space.","href":"companion/beastheart/bear/level-6/ursine-form/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Use Consumable","source":"other","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Unless otherwise noted in its description, a creature can activate a consumable treasure such as a potion with the Use Consumable maneuver. A creature can use this maneuver to administer a consumable treasure that benefits the user either to themself or to a willing adjacent creature. See Consumables in Chapter 13: Rewards.","href":"common/maneuvers/use-consumable/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Useful Emotion","klass":"Memonek","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Velloparatha—the worldsickness—might hinder you, but you know how to turn your pain into something your enemies feel. At the start of any combat, you gain 1 surge.","href":"trait/memonek/useful-emotion/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Vanishing Ward","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your ward allows you to slip away from threats. Whenever you take damage, you become invisible until the end of your next turn.","href":"talent/level-1/vanishing-ward/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Veiling Bed","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Void","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"The area provides concealment for you and your allies.","href":"elementalist/level-4/veiling-bed/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Vengeance Mark","klass":"Revenant","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Additionally, you have the following signature ability. Signature abilities can be used at will.","grants":"the Detonate Sigil main action","href":"trait/revenant/vengeance-mark/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Ventriloquist","klass":"Shadow","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you communicate, you can throw your voice so that it seems to originate from a creature or object within 10 squares. If you are hidden, talking this way doesn't cause you to be revealed.","href":"shadow/level-7/ventriloquist/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Verbal Duel","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Duelist","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"Once on each of your turns while the target of your Foil feature is adjacent to you, you can use a free maneuver to exchange words with them. Make an opposed Presence test with the target. Whoever gets the higher result can make a free strike, which deals psychic damage instead of its usual damage.","href":"troubadour/level-5/verbal-duel/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Vessel of Retribution","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":5,"action":"maneuver","cost":"9 Piety","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"You infuse yourself or an ally with the retributive energy of the gods, waiting to be unleashed.","href":"ability/conduit/level-5/vessel-of-retribution/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Violence Will Not Aid Thee","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"3 Piety","keywords":["Magic","Ranged","Strike"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"After some holy lightning, your enemy will think twice about their next attack.","href":"ability/conduit/level-1/violence-will-not-aid-thee/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Virtue","klass":"Censor","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have an epic resource called virtue. Each time you finish a respite, you gain virtue equal to the XP you gain. You can spend virtue on your abilities as if it were wrath.","href":"censor/level-10/virtue/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Virtuoso Features","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"Choose one of the following features.","options":2,"href":"troubadour/level-5/virtuoso-features/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Virtuoso Performances","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Virtuoso","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have the following performance abilities, which are usable with your Routines feature.","options":2,"href":"troubadour/level-1/virtuoso-performances/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Visceral Roar","klass":"Fury","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Ferocity","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"conditions":["dazed"],"distance":"**2** burst","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"The sound of the storm within you staggers your opponents.","href":"ability/fury/level-2/visceral-roar/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Viscous Fire","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Fire","Magic","Ranged","Strike"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"A jet of heavy fire erupts where you strike.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-1/viscous-fire/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Vision","klass":"Talent","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have an epic resource called vision. Each time you finish a respite, you gain vision equal to the XP you gain. You can spend vision on your abilities as if it were clarity.","href":"talent/level-10/vision/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Void: Acolyte of the Mystery","klass":"Elementalist","subclass":"Void","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You use your immersion in the mystery of void magic to expand the reach of that magic better than other mages. The distance of all your abilities that have the Magic, Ranged, and Void keywords increases by 2 squares.","href":"elementalist/level-1/void-acolyte-of-the-mystery/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Volatile Reagents","klass":"Shadow","subclass":"Caustic Alchemy","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"Whenever you take damage, each enemy adjacent to you takes fire, acid, or poison damage (your choice) equal to your Agility score.","href":"shadow/level-5/volatile-reagents/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Volcano's Embrace","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Essence","keywords":["Earth","Fire","Magic","Ranged","Strike"],"conditions":["restrained"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Wrap them up in fire and melting stone.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-2/volcanos-embrace/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Vow","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Paragon","source":"class","level":8,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your words take on the power of your deity, with all the authority that entails. If you convince a creature to take an oath, they can't break it for 7 days. If you take an oath, you can't break it for 7 days.","href":"censor/level-8/vow/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Vuken (Unbalancing Attack)","klass":"Vuken","source":"kit","level":0,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"conditions":["prone"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"A wild assault forces your foe onto their back.","href":"ability/Kits/vuken-unbalancing-attack/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Wall of Fire","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Essence","keywords":["Area","Fire","Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"**10** wall within **10**","targets":"Special","flavor":"A blazing, beautifully organized inferno erupts at your command.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-3/wall-of-fire/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Wall of Ice","klass":"Null","subclass":"Cryokinetic","source":"class","level":6,"action":"main","cost":"9 Discipline","keywords":["Area","Psionic","Ranged"],"conditions":["restrained","slowed"],"distance":"**10** wall within **10**","targets":"Special","flavor":"You create a wall of ice.","href":"ability/null/level-6/wall-of-ice/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Ward of Delightful Consequences","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"A protective field of void magic absorbs violence aimed at you, then lets you hurl it back at your enemies. The first time each round that you take damage, you gain 1 surge.","href":"elementalist/level-1/ward-of-delightful-consequences/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Ward of Excellent Protection","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You weave a shield of all the elements around yourself, channeling their full protective power. You have immunity to acid, cold, corruption, fire, lightning, poison, or sonic damage equal to your Reason score.","href":"elementalist/level-1/ward-of-excellent-protection/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Ward of Nature's Affection","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"The green energy writhing within your body allows you to produce powerful vines when you're in danger. Whenever a creature within a number of squares equal to your Reason score deals damage to you, you can use a free triggered action to slide that creature up to a number of squares equal to your Reason score.","href":"elementalist/level-1/ward-of-natures-affection/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Ward of Surprising Reactivity","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You use the magic of fire to create a ward of explosive energy. Whenever an adjacent creature deals damage to you, you can use a free triggered action to push that creature up to a number of squares equal to twice your Reason score.","href":"elementalist/level-1/ward-of-surprising-reactivity/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Warmaster","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":10,"action":"trait","flavor":"You have mastered the entirety of possible strategies and tactics. Whenever you or any ally makes an ability roll against a target marked by you, the character making the roll can roll three dice and choose which two to use.","href":"tactician/level-10/warmaster/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Warrior Priest (Weakening Brand)","klass":"Warrior Priest","source":"kit","level":0,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Magic","Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **1**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"The impact of your weapon brands your target for destruction.","href":"ability/Kits/warrior-priest-weakening-brand/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Warrior's Prayer","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Magic","Ranged","Strike"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"Your quickly uttered prayer lends aggressive divine energy to a friend engaged in melee.","href":"ability/conduit/level-1/warriors-prayer/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Watchdog","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Guardian","source":"class","level":2,"action":"trait","flavor":"You and your companion can't be surprised.","href":"beastheart/level-2/watchdog/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"We Can't Be Upstaged!","klass":"Troubadour","subclass":"Duelist","source":"class","level":5,"action":"none","keywords":["Area","Magic","Performance"],"distance":"**5** aura","targets":"Self and each ally in the area","flavor":"Swordplay so graceful it looks like you all practiced this.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-5/we-cant-be-upstaged/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"We Meet at Last","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":3,"action":"maneuver","cost":"7 Drama","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"You magically intertwine your fate with another creature—for better or worse.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-3/we-meet-at-last/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Web of All That's Come Before","klass":"Elementalist","source":"class","level":5,"action":"main","cost":"9 Essence","keywords":["Area","Green","Magic","Ranged"],"conditions":["restrained"],"distance":"**4** cube within **10**","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"Threads you've been weaving through your adventures create a vibrant, pearlescent web.","href":"ability/elementalist/level-5/web-of-all-thats-come-before/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Web Shot","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"maneuver","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Companion","Ranged","Weapon"],"conditions":["restrained"],"distance":"Ranged Slinger","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"Once on each of your turns while the spider is rampaging, you can shoot a web to a ceiling, wall, or sturdy object above you within 5 squares as a free maneuver. You can then fly in a straight line to any space within 5 squares of that object.","href":"companion/beastheart/spider/level-6/web-slinger/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Wellspring of Grace","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Life","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Piety","keywords":["Area","Magic"],"distance":"**3** aura","targets":"Each ally in the area","flavor":"A holy light is emitted from your body, healing your allies.","href":"ability/conduit/level-2/wellspring-of-grace/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Whirlwind (Extension of My Arm)","klass":"Whirlwind","source":"kit","level":0,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Melee","Strike","Weapon"],"distance":"Melee **3**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"When you draw your whip back after an attack, your enemy is drawn ever closer.","href":"ability/Kits/whirlwind-extension-of-my-arm/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Wild Hunt","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Spark","source":"class","level":9,"action":"main","cost":"11 Ferocity","keywords":["Area","Companion","Magic"],"distance":"**5** cube within **20**","targets":"Each enemy in the area","flavor":"Your companion summons a ravening pack of spectral ancestors to devour your foes.","href":"ability/beastheart/level-9/wild-hunt/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Wild Nature","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your bond with your companion has awakened ancient, predatory instincts in you that fuel your battle prowess. But you aren't bound by your primal passions. As you decide which of your instincts to ignore and which to hone to deadly sharpness, you choose a wild nature from the following options, each of which grants you a skill. (Quick Build: Punisher.)","href":"beastheart/level-1/wild-nature/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Wild Nature Maneuver","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"Your wild nature grants you a maneuver, as shown on the Wild Nature Maneuvers table. Each maneuver can be used only by the beastheart.","options":4,"href":"beastheart/level-1/wild-nature-maneuver/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Wild Nature Triggered Action","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":1,"action":"trait","flavor":"You and your companion both gain the benefits of the kit, with the following exceptions:","options":6,"href":"beastheart/level-1/wild-nature-triggered-action/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Wild Rush","klass":"Beastheart","source":"class","level":6,"action":"trait","flavor":"While the boar is rampaging, you can use their Gore maneuver, and you and the boar can shift instead of move when using the Charge action or the Gore maneuver.","href":"companion/beastheart/boar/level-6/wild-rush/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Wildfire Pyre","klass":"Beastheart","subclass":"Spark","source":"class","level":5,"action":"trait","flavor":"When you or your companion deals damage with your Pyre ability, each enemy adjacent to either of you takes the damage.","href":"beastheart/level-5/wildfire-pyre/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Win This Day!","klass":"Tactician","source":"class","level":5,"action":"main","cost":"9 Focus","keywords":["Area"],"conditions":["prone"],"distance":"**3** burst","targets":"Self and each ally in the area","flavor":"You inspire your allies to recover and gather their strength.","href":"ability/tactician/level-5/win-this-day/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Windwalk","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Storm","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"While you have 5 or more Victories, you can fly. If you can already fly, you have a +2 bonus to speed while flying instead.","href":"censor/level-4/windwalk/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Wings","klass":"Devil","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"You possess wings powerful enough to take you airborne. While using your wings to fly, you can stay aloft for a number of rounds equal to your Might score (minimum 1 round) before you fall. While using your wings to fly at 3rd level or lower, you have damage weakness 5.","href":"trait/devil/wings/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Wings","klass":"Dragon Knight","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"You possess wings powerful enough to take you airborne. While using your wings to fly, you can stay aloft for a number of rounds equal to your Might score (minimum 1 round) before you fall. While using your wings to fly at 3rd level or lower, you have damage weakness 5.","href":"trait/dragon-knight/wings/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"With My Blessing","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Oracle","source":"class","level":2,"action":"main","cost":"5 Wrath","keywords":["Magic","Ranged"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"Self or one ally","flavor":"A word in prayer, and the gods show the way.","href":"ability/censor/level-2/with-my-blessing/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Wither","klass":"Conduit","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Magic","Ranged","Strike"],"distance":"Ranged **10**","targets":"One creature or object","flavor":"A bolt of holy energy saps the life from a foe.","href":"ability/conduit/level-1/wither/"},{"kind":"ability","name":"Witty Banter","klass":"Troubadour","source":"class","level":1,"action":"main","cost":"Signature","keywords":["Magic","Melee","Ranged","Strike"],"distance":"Melee **1** or ranged **5**","targets":"One creature","flavor":"A lyrical (and physical) jab insults an enemy and inspires an ally.","href":"ability/troubadour/level-1/witty-banter/"},{"kind":"trait","name":"Wode Elf Traits","klass":"Wode Elf","source":"ancestry","level":0,"action":"trait","flavor":"Wode elf heroes have access to the following traits.","options":2,"href":"trait/wode-elf/wode-elf-traits/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Wode Road","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Nature","source":"class","level":4,"action":"trait","flavor":"As a main action, you touch a living tree and make it part of a divine transportation network. You can maintain a number of trees in your network equal to your Presence score. Whenever you touch any tree in your network, you can use a main action to teleport yourself and any willing creatures within 10 squares of you to a tree in your network on the same world. If a tree in your network dies, it is no longer part of the network. You can remove a tree from your network no matter your distance from it, including across different worlds (no action required).","href":"censor/level-4/wode-road/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Word of Death Deferred","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Death","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can stop death from taking your allies. When an ally within distance of your My Life for Yours ability dies and you are not dying, you can use a free triggered action to instead have that ally fall unconscious until they regain Stamina.","href":"censor/level-7/word-of-death-deferred/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Word of Death Deferred","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Death","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"You can stop death from taking your allies. When an ally within distance of your Healing Grace ability dies and you are not dying, you can use a free triggered action to instead have that ally fall unconscious until they regain Stamina.","href":"conduit/level-7/word-of-death-deferred/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Word of Fate Denied","klass":"Censor","subclass":"Fate","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"When an ally within 10 squares takes damage that would leave them dying, you can use a free triggered action to make yourself or another willing creature within 10 squares of you the target of the triggering damage instead. The creature you choose takes the damage and suffers any effects associated with it, and that damage can't be reduced in any way.","href":"censor/level-7/word-of-fate-denied/"},{"kind":"feature","name":"Word of Fate Denied","klass":"Conduit","subclass":"Fate","source":"class","level":7,"action":"trait","flavor":"When an ally within 10 squares takes damage that would leave them dying, you can use a free triggered action to make yourself or another willing creature within 10 squares of you the target of the triggering damage instead.
